        self.ws_current_date_data.ws_current_time = int(now.strftime("%H%M%S%f")[:8])

def calculate_premiums():
    """4200-CALCULATE-PREMIUMS.
    4201    DISPLAY "CALCULATING PREMIUMS..."
    4202    SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    4203    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
    4204        READ INSURANCE-MASTER NEXT
    4205            AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
    4206            NOT AT END
    4207                PERFORM 4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM
    4208                PERFORM 4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR
    4209                PERFORM 4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM
    4210        END-READ
    4211    END-PERFORM.
    """
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        insurance_master = read_insurance_master()
        if insurance_master is None:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            determine_base_premium(insurance_master)
            apply_risk_factor(insurance_master)
            calculate_final_premium(insurance_master)

def read_insurance_master() -> InsuranceMaster | None:
    """Dummy function to simulate reading InsuranceMaster record"""
    return InsuranceMaster()

def determine_base_premium(insurance_master: InsuranceMaster):
    """4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM.
    4211    EVALUATE TRUE
    4212        WHEN INS-LIFE
    4213            COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
    4214                INS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT / 1000 * WS-LIFE-RATE-PER-1000
    4215        WHEN INS-HEALTH
    4216            MOVE WS-HEALTH-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
    4217        WHEN INS-AUTO
    4218            MOVE WS-AUTO-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
    4219        WHEN INS-HOME
    4220            COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
    4221                INS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT / 1000 * WS-HOME-RATE-PER-1000
    4222        WHEN INS-UMBRELLA
    4223            MOVE WS-UMBRELLA-RATE TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
    4224    END-EVALUATE.
    """
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    match True:
        case insurance_master.ins_life:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_master.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
        case insurance_master.ins_health:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
        case insurance_master.ins_auto:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
        case insurance_master.ins_home:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_master.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
        case insurance_master.ins_umbrella:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_UMBRELLA_RATE

def apply_risk_factor(insurance_master: InsuranceMaster):
    """4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR.
    4221    IF INS-CLAIMS-COUNT > 2
    4222        COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = WS-CALC-AMOUNT * 1.25
    4223    END-IF.
    """
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if insurance_master.ins_claims_count > 2:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal("1.25")

def calculate_final_premium(insurance_master: InsuranceMaster):
    """4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM.
    4231    MOVE WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO INS-PREMIUM-AMOUNT
    4232    ADD WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-PREMIUMS.
    """
    global WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS
    insurance_master.ins_premium_amount = WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def calculate_position_value(investment_master: InvestmentMaster):
    """5210-CALCULATE-POSITION-VALUE.
    5211    COMPUTE INV-MARKET-VALUE = 
    5212        INV-QUANTITY * INV-CURRENT-PRICE.
    """
    investment_master.inv_market_value = investment_master.inv_quantity * investment_master.inv_current_price

def calculate_gain_loss(investment_master: InvestmentMaster):
    """5220-CALCULATE-GAIN-LOSS.
    5221    COMPUTE INV-GAIN-LOSS = 
    5222        INV-MARKET-VALUE - (INV-QUANTITY * INV-PURCHASE-PRICE).
    """
    investment_master.inv_gain_loss = investment_master.inv_market_value - (investment_master.inv_quantity * investment_master.inv_purchase_price)

    """5230-UPDATE-TOTALS.

    """
    global WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += investment_master.inv_market_value

    8501    EVALUATE TRUE
    8502        WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-1-MAX
    8503            COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
    8504                WS-CALC-AMOUNT * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE
    8505        WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-2-MAX
    8506            COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
    8507                (WS-BRACKET-1-MAX * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE) +
    8508                ((WS-CALC-AMOUNT - WS-BRACKET-1-MAX) * 
    8509                 WS-BRACKET-2-RATE)
    8510        WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-3-MAX
    8511            COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
    8512                (WS-BRACKET-1-MAX * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE) +
    8513                ((WS-BRACKET-2-MAX - WS-BRACKET-1-MAX) * 
    8514                 WS-BRACKET-2-RATE) +
    8515                ((WS-CALC-AMOUNT - WS-BRACKET-2-MAX) * 
    8516                 WS-BRACKET-3-RATE)
    8517        WHEN OTHER
    8518            COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
    8519                WS-CALC-AMOUNT * WS-BRACKET-5-RATE
    8520    END-EVALUATE.
    """
    global WS_CALC_TAX
    match True:
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                          ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                          ((WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + \
                          ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
        case _:
            WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

def termination():
    """9000-TERMINATION.
    9001    PERFORM 9100-CLOSE-FILES
    9002    PERFORM 9200-DISPLAY-STATISTICS
    9003    DISPLAY "MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY".
    """
    close_files()
    display_statistics()
    print("MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY")

def close_files():
    """9100-CLOSE-FILES.
    9101    CLOSE CUSTOMER-MASTER
    9102    CLOSE ACCOUNT-MASTER
    9103    CLOSE LOAN-MASTER
    9104    CLOSE INSURANCE-MASTER
    9105    CLOSE INVESTMENT-MASTER
    9106    CLOSE TRANSACTION-LOG
    9107    CLOSE AUDIT-TRAIL
    9108    CLOSE REPORT-FILE.
    """
    close_customer_master()
    close_account_master()
    close_loan_master()
    close_insurance_master()
    close_investment_master()
    close_transaction_log()
    close_audit_trail()
    close_report_file()

def display_statistics():
    """9200-DISPLAY-STATISTICS.
    9201    DISPLAY "============================================"
    9202    DISPLAY "       PROCESSING STATISTICS                "
    9203    DISPLAY "============================================"
    9204    MOVE WS-CUST-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
    9205    DISPLAY "CUSTOMERS PROCESSED:    " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
    9206    MOVE WS-ACCT-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
    9207    DISPLAY "ACCOUNTS PROCESSED:     " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
    9208    MOVE WS-TRAN-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
    9209    DISPLAY "TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
    9210    MOVE WS-LOAN-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
    9211    DISPLAY "LOANS PROCESSED:        " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
    9212    MOVE WS-ERROR-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
    9213    DISPLAY "ERRORS ENCOUNTERED:     " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
    921

class Data:
    WS_ANNUAL_FEE_CARD: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    WS_TOTAL_FEES: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    WS_WIRE_FEE_DOMESTIC: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')

    WS_CALC_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')

    WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    WS_NOT_APPROVED: bool = False
    WS_EOF: bool = False
    WS_NOT_EOF: bool = False
    WS_SAVINGS_RATE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    WS_PERSONAL_RATE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')

    CUSTOMER_MASTER: str = ""

    CUST_CREDIT_SCORE: int = 0

    CUST_LAST_ACTIVITY: int = 0
    WS_CURRENT_DATE: int = 0
    WS_ERROR_COUNT: int = 0
    WS_PROCESS_COUNT: int = 0
    CUST_NAME: str = ""
    CUST_STATE: str = ""
    CUST_LAST_NAME: str = ""

def customer_segmentation(data: Data) -> None:
    """9310-CUSTOMER-SEGMENTATION."""
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not data.WS_EOF:
        # Simulate READ CUSTOMER-MASTER NEXT
        # In real implementation, read from file
        if not data.CUSTOMER_MASTER:  # Simulating AT END
            data.WS_EOF = True
        else:  # Simulating NOT AT END
            calculate_clv(data)
            assign_segment(data)

def calculate_clv(data: Data) -> None:
    """9311-CALCULATE-CLV."""
    data.WS_CALC_RESULT = (data.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE * data.WS_SAVINGS_RATE) + (data.CUST_TOTAL_LOANS * data.WS_PERSONAL_RATE) + (data.CUST_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS * Decimal('0.01'))

def assign_segment(data: Data) -> None:
    """9312-ASSIGN-SEGMENT."""
    if data.WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000:
        data.WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM'
    elif data.WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
        data.WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD'
    elif data.WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
        data.WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER'
    else:
        data.WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE'

def sweep_accounts(data: Data) -> None:
    """9632-SWEEP-ACCOUNTS."""
    if data.ACCT_BALANCE > data.ACCT_MIN_BALANCE:
        data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = data.ACCT_BALANCE - data.ACCT_MIN_BALANCE
        data.ACCT_BALANCE -= data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        data.WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def extract_data(data: Data) -> None:
    """A110-EXTRACT-DATA."""
    data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not data.WS_EOF:
        # Simulate READ CUSTOMER-MASTER NEXT
        # In real implementation, read from file
        if not data.CUSTOMER_MASTER:  # Simulating AT END
            data.WS_EOF = True
        else:  # Simulating NOT AT END
            data.WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1

def transform_data(data: Data) -> None:
    """A120-TRANSFORM-DATA."""
    cleanse_data(data)
    standardize_data(data)
    enrich_data(data)

def cleanse_data(data: Data) -> None:
    """A121-CLEANSE-DATA."""
    if data.CUST_NAME == "":
        data.CUST_LAST_NAME = "UNKNOWN"

    """A122-STANDARDIZE-DATA."""

    """A210-COMPLETENESS-CHECK."""
    if data.CUST_ID == "":

    """A220-ACCURACY-CHECK."""
    if data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE < 300 or data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 850:
        data.WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1

        pass

@dataclass
class DataStructure:
    WS_TEMP_CODE: str = ""
    CUST_SSN: str = ""
    CUST_STATUS: str = ""
    TRAN_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    CUST_CREDIT_SCORE: int = 0
    CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    CUST_TOTAL_LOANS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_CALC_RESULT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_CALC_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TOTAL_FEES: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_ATM_FEE_FOREIGN: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_PROCESS-COUNT: int = 0
    WS_ERROR_COUNT: int = 0
    WS_EOF: bool = False
    WS_NOT_EOF: bool = False
    CUST_RISK_RATING: str = ""
    WS_VALID: bool = False
    LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    LOAN_PAID_OFF: bool = False
    WS_FORMATTED_COUNT: str = ""
    WS_CUST_COUNT: int = 0
    WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP: str = ""
    WS_TEMP_STRING: str = ""

def C100_TRANSACTION_MONITORING(data: DataStructure):
    """C100-TRANSACTION-MONITORING."""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")  # DISPLAY "MONITORING TRANSACTIONS..."
    data.WS_NOT_EOF = True  # SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    while not data.WS_EOF:  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        # READ TRANSACTION-LOG NEXT
        if True: #placeholder for reading transaction log
            data.WS_EOF = True # SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
        else:
            C110_RULE_BASED_DETECTION(data)  # PERFORM C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION
            C120_BEHAVIOR_ANALYSIS(data)  # PERFORM C120-BEHAVIOR-ANALYSIS
            C130_NETWORK_ANALYSIS(data)  # PERFORM C130-NETWORK-ANALYSIS

def C110_RULE_BASED_DETECTION(data: DataStructure):
    """C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION."""
    if data.TRAN_AMOUNT >= 10000:  # IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 10000
        C111_FLAG_CTR(data)  # PERFORM C111-FLAG-CTR
    if data.TRAN_AMOUNT >= 5000 and data.TRAN_AMOUNT < 10000:  # IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 5000 AND TRAN-AMOUNT < 10000
        C112_CHECK_STRUCTURING(data)  # PERFORM C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING

def C111_FLAG_CTR(data: DataStructure):
    """C111-FLAG-CTR."""
    data.WS_PROCESS-COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-PROCESS-COUNT.

def C112_CHECK_STRUCTURING(data: DataStructure):
    """C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING."""
    data.WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-ERROR-COUNT.

def D110_CLASSIFICATION(data: DataStructure):
    """D110-CLASSIFICATION."""
    if data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 750:  # IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 750
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'A'  # MOVE 'A' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    elif data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 650:  # ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 650
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'B'  # MOVE 'B' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    elif data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 550:  # ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 550
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'C'  # MOVE 'C' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    else:  # ELSE
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'D'  # MOVE 'D' TO CUST-RISK-RATING

def D120_REGRESSION(data: DataStructure):
    """D120-REGRESSION."""
    data.WS_CALC_RESULT = (data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE * 10) + \
                         (data.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE / 1000) - \
                         (data.CUST_TOTAL_LOANS / 2000)  # COMPUTE WS-CALC-RESULT = (CUST-CREDIT-SCORE * 10) + (CUST-TOTAL-BALANCE / 1000) - (CUST-TOTAL-LOANS / 2000).

@dataclass
class Tables:
    """Structure for COBOL Tables"""
    rate_table_entry: list[RateTableEntry]
    branch_table_entry: list[BranchTableEntry]
    exception_entry: list[str]
    audit_entry: list[str]

class CobolProgram:

    def __init__(self):
        self.customer_master: CustomerMaster = CustomerMaster()
        self.ws_work_areas: WorkAreas = WorkAreas()
        self.ws_counters: Counters = Counters()
        self.ws_totals: Totals = Totals()
        self.tables: Tables = Tables(rate_table_entry=[RateTableEntry() for _ in range(100)],
                                    branch_table_entry=[BranchTableEntry() for _ in range(50)],
                                    exception_entry = ['' for _ in range(100)], #Example Size
                                    audit_entry = ['' for _ in range(100)]) #Example Size
        self.account_record: Optional[AccountFileRecord] = None
        self.report_header: Optional[ReportHeader] = None
        self.report_detail: Optional[ReportDetail] = None
        self.rate_value: list[Decimal] = [Decimal('0.00') for _ in range(100)] #Example size
        self.txn_account_id: str = ''
        self.txn_amount: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.txn_type: str = ''
        self.txn_target_account: str = ''
        self.audit_record: Optional[WsAuditRecord] = None
        self.ws_audit_record: Optional[WsAuditRecord] = None
        self.alert_record: Optional[WsAlertRecord] = None
        self.audit_entry: list[str] = ['' for _ in range(100)] #Example Size
        self.error_record: Optional[ErrorFileRecord] = None
        self.exception_entry: list[str] = ['' for _ in range(100)] #Example Size
        self.batch_header_record: Optional[BatchHeaderRecord] = None
        self.batch_item_record: Optional[BatchItemRecord] = None
        self.rejection_record: Optional[WsRejectionRecord] = None

    def main_loop(self):
        """
           SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
               READ CUSTOMER-MASTER NEXT
                   AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
                   NOT AT END
                       PERFORM I110-UPDATE-PROFILE
                       PERFORM I120-ENRICH-PROFILE
                       ADD 1 TO WS-CUST-COUNT
               END-READ
           END-PERFORM.
        """
        self.ws_work_areas.ws_not_eof = True
        while not self.ws_work_areas.ws_eof:
            try:
                # Assuming read_customer_master() is a function that reads the next customer record
                self.customer_master = self.read_customer_master()
                self.i110_update_profile()
                self.i120_enrich_profile()
                self.ws_work_areas.ws_cust_count += 1
            except StopIteration:
                self.ws_work_areas.ws_eof = True

    def i110_update_profile(self):
        """
           I110-UPDATE-PROFILE.
           MOVE WS-CURRENT-DATE TO CUST-LAST-ACTIVITY.
        """
        # Assuming CUST-LAST-ACTIVITY is a field in customer_master
        self.customer_master.cust_last_activity = self.ws_work_areas.ws_current_date

    def main_control(self):
        """
           0000-MAIN-CONTROL.
           PERFORM 1000-INITIALIZATION
           PERFORM 2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS
              UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
           PERFORM 9000-FINALIZATION
           STOP RUN.
        """
        self.initialization()
        while self.ws_work_areas.ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
            self.process_transactions()
        self.finalization()
        # STOP RUN is equivalent to program termination in COBOL, so no explicit action is needed here

    def initialization(self):
        """
           1000-INITIALIZATION.
           INITIALIZE WS-WORK-AREAS
           INITIALIZE WS-COUNTERS
           INITIALIZE WS-TOTALS
           MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-CURRENT-DATETIME
           MOVE WS-CURR-YEAR  TO RPT-YEAR
           MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO RPT-MONTH
           MOVE WS-CURR-DAY   TO RPT-DAY
           PERFORM 1100-OPEN-FILES
           PERFORM 1200-READ-PARAMETERS
           PERFORM 1300-INITIALIZE-TABLES
           PERFORM 1400-LOAD-REFERENCE-DATA.
        """
        self.ws_work_areas = WorkAreas()
        self.ws_counters = Counters()
        self.ws_totals = Totals()
        # Assuming we can get current date as string
        import datetime
        now = datetime.datetime.now()
        self.ws_work_areas.ws_current_datetime = now.strftime("%Y%m%d")
        self.report_header = ReportHeader(rpt_year=now.strftime("%Y"), 
                                        rpt_month=now.strftime("%m"), 
                                        rpt_day=now.strftime("%d"))
        self.open_files()
        self.read_parameters()
        self.initialize_tables()
        self.load_reference_data()

    def open_files(self):
        """
           1100-OPEN-FILES.
           OPEN INPUT  CUSTOMER-FILE
           OPEN INPUT  ACCOUNT-FILE
           OPEN INPUT  TRANSACTION-FILE
           OPEN OUTPUT REPORT-FILE
           OPEN OUTPUT ERROR-FILE
           OPEN I-O    MASTER-FILE
           IF WS-FILE-STATUS NOT = '00'
              MOVE 'FILE OPEN ERROR' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
              PERFORM 9500-ABORT-PROCESS
           END-IF.
        """
        # Placeholder for file operations
        try:
            self.customer_file = open("CUSTOMER-FILE", "r")
            self.account_file = open("ACCOUNT-FILE", "r")
            self.transaction_file = open("TRANSACTION-FILE", "r")
            self.report_file = open("REPORT-FILE", "w")
            self.error_file = open("ERROR-FILE", "w")
            self.master_file = open("MASTER-FILE", "r+")  # I-O mode

            self.ws_work_areas.ws_file_status = '00' #Setting to success

        except Exception as e:
            self.ws_work_areas.ws_file_status = '99' #Setting to error
            self.ws_work_areas.ws_error_msg = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'
            self.abort_process()

    def read_parameters(self):
        """
           1200-READ-PARAMETERS.
           ACCEPT WS-PARAM-DATE FROM DATE
           ACCEPT WS-PARAM-TIME FROM TIME
           MOVE 'BATCH-001' TO WS-JOB-ID
           MOVE 'PRODUCTION' TO WS-ENV-TYPE
           COMPUTE WS-PROCESS-DATE = 
              FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PARAM-DATE).
        """
        import datetime
        now = datetime.datetime.now()
        self.ws_work_areas.ws_param_date = now.strftime("%Y%m%d")
        self.ws_work_areas.ws_param_time = now.strftime("%H%M%S")
        self.ws_work_areas.ws_job_id = 'BATCH-001'
        self.ws_work_areas.ws_env_type = 'PRODUCTION'
        self.ws_work_areas.ws_process_date = int(now.strftime("%Y%m%d"))  # INTEGER-OF-DATE equivalent

    def initialize_tables(self):
        """
           1300-INITIALIZE-TABLES.
           PERFORM VARYING WS-TBL-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
              UNTIL WS-TBL-IDX > 100
              INITIALIZE RATE-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX)
              MOVE ZEROES TO RT-RATE(WS-TBL-IDX)
              MOVE SPACES TO RT-CODE(WS-TBL-IDX)
           END-PERFORM
           PERFORM VARYING WS-TBL-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
              UNTIL WS-TBL-IDX > 50
              INITIALIZE BRANCH-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX)
           END-PERFORM.
        """
        for self.ws_work_areas.ws_tbl_idx in range(1, 101):
            self.tables.rate_table_entry[self.ws_work_areas.ws_tbl_idx - 1] = RateTableEntry(rt_rate=Decimal('0.00'), rt_code='')

        for self.ws_work_areas.ws_tbl_idx in range(1, 51):
            self.tables.branch_table_entry[self.ws_work_areas.ws_tbl_idx - 1] = BranchTableEntry()

    def load_reference_data(self):
        """
           1400-LOAD-REFERENCE-DATA.
           MOVE 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y' 
                      OR WS-TBL-IDX > 100
              READ REFERENCE-FILE INTO WS-REF-RECORD
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    MOVE WS-REF-CODE TO RT-CODE(WS-TBL-IDX)
                    MOVE WS-REF-RATE TO RT-RATE(WS-TBL-IDX)
                    ADD 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
        """
        self.ws_work_areas.ws_tbl_idx = 1
        try:
            while self.ws_work_areas.ws_eof_flag != 'Y' and self.ws_work_areas.ws_tbl_idx <= 100:
                # Assuming read_reference_file() is a function that reads the next reference record
                ws_ref_record = self.read_reference_file()
                self.tables.rate_table_entry[self.ws_work_areas.ws_tbl_idx - 1].rt_code = ws_ref_record.ws_ref_code
                self.tables.rate_table_entry[self.ws_work_areas.ws_tbl_idx - 1].rt_rate = ws_ref_record.ws_ref_rate
                self.ws_work_areas.ws_tbl_idx += 1
        except StopIteration:
            self.ws_work_areas.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        self.ws_work_areas.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    def process_transactions(self):
        """
           2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS.
           READ TRANSACTION-FILE INTO WS-TRANSACTION-REC
              AT END
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
              NOT AT END
                 ADD 1 TO WS-TRANS-COUNT
                 PERFORM 2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION
                 IF WS-VALID-FLAG = 'Y'
                    PERFORM 2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE
                 ELSE
                    PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR
                 END-IF
           END-READ.
        """
        try:
            # Assuming read_transaction_file() is a function that reads the next transaction record
            ws_transaction_rec = self.read_transaction_file()
            self.txn_account_id = ws_transaction_rec.txn_account_id
            self.txn_amount = ws_transaction_rec.txn_amount
            self.txn_type = ws_transaction_rec.txn_type
            self.txn_target_account = ws_transaction_rec.txn_target_account

            self.ws_work_areas.ws_trans_count += 1
            self.validate_transaction()
            if self.ws_work_areas.ws_valid_flag == 'Y':
                self.process_by_type()
            else:
                self.handle_error()
        except StopIteration:
            self.ws_work_areas.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    def validate_transaction(self):
        """
           2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION.
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
           IF TXN-ACCOUNT-ID = SPACES OR LOW-VALUES
              MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
              MOVE 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
              EXIT PARAGRAPH
           END-IF
           IF TXN-AMOUNT IS NOT NUMERIC
              MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
              MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
              EXIT PARAGRAPH
           END-IF
           IF TXN-TYPE NOT = 'D' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'W'
              AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'T' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'I'
              MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
              MOVE 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
           END-IF
           PERFORM 2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS
           PERFORM 2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES.
        """
        self.ws_work_areas.ws_valid_flag = 'Y'
        if self.txn_account_id == '' or self.txn_account_id is None:  # Assuming SPACES is an empty string and LOW-VALUES is None
            self.ws_work_areas.ws_valid_flag = 'N'
            self.ws_work_areas.ws_error_msg = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
            return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
        try:
            float(self.txn_amount)
        except ValueError:
            self.ws_work_areas.ws_valid_flag = 'N'
            self.ws_work_areas.ws_error_msg = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
            return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
        if self.

# === CHUNK 6 (lines 3341-4008) ===
def __evaluate_interest_rate(self):
    """
    0000 MOVE 2.0 TO WS-INTEREST-RATE
    """
    match True:
        case _:
            self.ws_interest_rate = Decimal('2.5')


def calculate_simple_interest(self):
    """
    7200-CALCULATE-SIMPLE-INTEREST.
    """
    self.ws_simple_interest = (
        self.ws_account_balance * self.ws_interest_rate * self.ws_days_in_period / Decimal('36500')
    )

def calculate_compound_interest(self):
    """
    7300-CALCULATE-COMPOUND-INTEREST.
    """
    self.ws_compound_factor = (
        (1 + self.ws_interest_rate / Decimal('36500')) ** self.ws_days_in_period
    )
    self.ws_compound_interest = (
        self.ws_account_balance * (self.ws_compound_factor - 1)
    )

def apply_interest(self):
    """
    7400-APPLY-INTEREST.
    """
    if self.ws_interest_method == 'S':
        self.ws_account_balance += self.ws_simple_interest
    else:
        self.ws_account_balance += self.ws_compound_interest
    self.update_account()

def fee_processing(self):
    """
    8000-FEE-PROCESSING.
    """
    self.calculate_monthly_fee()
    self.calculate_transaction_fees()
    self.apply_fee_waivers()
    self.deduct_fees()

def calculate_monthly_fee(self):
    """
    8100-CALCULATE-MONTHLY-FEE.
    """
    match self.ws_account_type:
        case 'CHK':
            self.ws_monthly_fee = Decimal('12.00')
        case 'SAV':
            self.ws_monthly_fee = Decimal('5.00')
        case 'PRM':
            self.ws_monthly_fee = Decimal('25.00')
        case _:
            self.ws_monthly_fee = Decimal('0.00')

def calculate_transaction_fees(self):
    """
    8200-CALCULATE-TRANSACTION-FEES.
    """
    if self.ws_trans_count > self.ws_free_trans_limit:
        self.ws_excess_trans = self.ws_trans_count - self.ws_free_trans_limit
        self.ws_trans_fee = self.ws_excess_trans * self.ws_per_trans_fee
    else:
        self.ws_trans_fee = Decimal('0.00')

def apply_fee_waivers(self):
    """
    8300-APPLY-FEE-WAIVERS.
    """
    if self.ws_account_balance >= self.ws_min_balance_waiver:
        self.ws_monthly_fee = Decimal('0.00')
    if self.ws_customer_tier == 'GOLD' or self.ws_customer_tier == 'PLATINUM':
        self.ws_trans_fee = self.ws_trans_fee * Decimal('0.5')

def deduct_fees(self):
    """
    8400-DEDUCT-FEES.
    """
    self.ws_total_fees = self.ws_monthly_fee + self.ws_trans_fee
    self.ws_account_balance -= self.ws_total_fees
    self.update_account()
    self.record_fee_transaction()

def record_fee_transaction(self):
    """
    8450-RECORD-FEE-TRANSACTION.
    """
    self.ws_fee_record = WSFeeRecord()
    self.ws_fee_record.fee_account = self.txn_account_id
    self.ws_fee_record.fee_amount = self.ws_total_fees
    self.ws_fee_record.fee_description = 'MONTHLY FEE'
    self.ws_fee_record.fee_date = date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")  #FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
    self.write_fee_record(self.ws_fee_record) # Assuming write_fee_record is defined elsewhere

def finalization(self):
    """
    9000-FINALIZATION.
    """
    self.write_control_totals()
    self.close_files()
    self.display_summary()

def write_control_totals(self):
    """
    9100-WRITE-CONTROL-TOTALS.
    """
    self.ws_control_record = WSControlRecord()
    self.ws_control_record.ctl_trans_count = self.ws_trans_count
    self.ws_control_record.ctl_deposits = self.ws_total_deposits
    self.ws_control_record.ctl_withdrawals = self.ws_total_withdrawals
    self.ws_control_record.ctl_error_count = self.ws_error_count
    self.ws_control_record.ctl_run_date = date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d") #FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
    self.write_control_record(self.ws_control_record) # Assuming write_control_record is defined elsewhere

def close_files(self):
    """
    9200-CLOSE-FILES.
    """
    self.close_customer_file()
    self.close_account_file()
    self.close_transaction_file()
    self.close_report_file()
    self.close_error_file()
    self.close_master_file()

def display_summary(self):
    """
    9300-DISPLAY-SUMMARY.
    """
    print('==========================================')
    print('MEGA-ENTERPRISE PROCESSING COMPLETE')
    print('==========================================')
    print(f'TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED:  {self.ws_trans_count}')
    print(f'DEPOSITS:               {self.ws_deposit_count}')
    print(f'WITHDRAWALS:            {self.ws_withdrawal_count}')
    print(f'TRANSFERS:              {self.ws_transfer_count}')
    print(f'ERRORS:                 {self.ws_error_count}')
    print(f'TOTAL DEPOSITS:    ${self.ws_total_deposits}')
    print(f'TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: ${self.ws_total_withdrawals}')
    print(f'NET CHANGE:        ${self.ws_net_change}')
    print('==========================================')

def abort_process(self):
    """
    9500-ABORT-PROCESS.
    """
    print(f'CRITICAL ERROR: {self.ws_abort_reason}')
    print(f'PROCESSING ABORTED AT {date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")}') #FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
    self.close_files()
    #STOP RUN WITH STATUS 8 (Implementation depends on the environment)
    exit(8)

    ws_bonds_pct: Decimal = Decimal('0.00') # 10 WS-BONDS-PCT         PIC 9(03)V99.
    ws_cash_pct: Decimal = Decimal('0.00') # 10 WS-CASH-PCT          PIC 9(03)V99.
    ws_real_estate_pct: Decimal = Decimal('0.00') # 10 WS-REAL-ESTATE-PCT   PIC 9(03)V99.
    ws_other_pct: Decimal = Decimal('0.00') # 10 WS-OTHER-PCT         PIC 9(03)V99.

# === CHUNK 7 (lines 4009-4676) ===
@dataclass
class AmortizationEntry:
    payment_num: int
    payment_amt: Decimal
    interest: Decimal
    principal: Decimal
    escrow: Decimal
    total_pmt: Decimal
    balance: Decimal
    payment_date: int

def calculate_pmi(self):
    """10335-CALCULATE-PMI."""
    match True:
        case self.ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
            self.ws_pmi_amount = (
                self.ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0125") / 12
            )
        case self.ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
            self.ws_pmi_amount = (
                self.ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0100") / 12
            )
        case self.ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
            self.ws_pmi_amount = (
                self.ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0075") / 12
            )
        case _:
            self.ws_pmi_amount = (
                self.ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0050") / 12
            )

def evaluate_history(self):
    """10340-EVALUATE-HISTORY."""
    if self.ws_late_90_days > 0:
        self.ws_risk_score -= 50
        self.ws_factor_1 = "SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY"
    if self.ws_late_60_days > 2:
        self.ws_risk_score -= 30
        self.ws_factor_2 = "60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES"
    if self.ws_late_30_days > 5:
        self.ws_risk_score -= 20
        self.ws_factor_3 = "MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES"

def calculate_final_risk(self):
    """10350-CALCULATE-FINAL-RISK."""
    self.ws_risk_score = self.ws_risk_score / 4
    match True:
        case self.ws_risk_score >= 80:
            self.ws_risk_category = "LOW RISK"
        case self.ws_risk_score >= 60:
            self.ws_risk_category = "MODERATE"
        case self.ws_risk_score >= 40:
            self.ws_risk_category = "ELEVATED"
        case _:
            self.ws_risk_category = "HIGH RISK"

def determine_approval(self):
    """10400-DETERMINE-APPROVAL."""
    if self.ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        self.ws_approval_status = 'D'
        self.ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return
    if self.ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        self.ws_approval_status = 'D'
        self.ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return
    if self.ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        self.ws_approval_status = 'D'
        self.ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return
    self.ws_approval_status = 'A'
    self.calculate_approved_terms()

def calculate_approved_terms(self):
    """10450-CALCULATE-APPROVED-TERMS."""
    self.ws_approved_amount = self.ws_loan_amount
    match self.ws_credit_tier:
        case 'A':
            self.ws_approved_rate = self.ws_base_rate + Decimal("0.00")
        case 'B':
            self.ws_approved_rate = self.ws_base_rate + Decimal("0.50")
        case 'C':
            self.ws_approved_rate = self.ws_base_rate + Decimal("1.50")
        case 'D':
            self.ws_approved_rate = self.ws_base_rate + Decimal("3.00")
    if self.ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
        self.ws_approved_rate += Decimal("0.50")

def generate_loan_terms(self):
    """10500-GENERATE-LOAN-TERMS."""
    self.ws_loan_interest_rate = self.ws_approved_rate
    self.ws_monthly_rate = self.ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    self.ws_compound_factor = (1 + self.ws_monthly_rate) ** self.ws_loan_term_months
    self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt = (
        self.ws_loan_amount
        * self.ws_monthly_rate
        * self.ws_compound_factor
        / (self.ws_compound_factor - 1)
    )
    self.ws_loan_principal_bal = self.ws_loan_amount

def create_amortization(self):
    """10600-CREATE-AMORTIZATION."""
    self.ws_running_balance = self.ws_loan_amount
    self.ws_payment_date = date.today()
    self.amortization_schedule = []
    for self.ws_amort_idx in range(1, self.ws_loan_term_months + 1):
        amortization_entry = self.calculate_payment_split()
        self.amortization_schedule.append(amortization_entry)

def calculate_payment_split(self) -> AmortizationEntry:
    """10650-CALCULATE-PAYMENT-SPLIT."""
    amort_interest = self.ws_running_balance * self.ws_monthly_rate
    amort_principal = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest
    self.ws_running_balance -= amort_principal
    amort_balance = self.ws_running_balance
    amort_payment_num = self.ws_amort_idx
    amort_payment_amt = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt

    if self.loan_mortgage:
        amort_escrow = (self.ws_property_tax + self.ws_insurance_premium) / 12
        amort_total_pmt = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow + self.ws_pmi_amount
    else:
        amort_total_pmt = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt

    self.advance_payment_date()
    amort_payment_date = (
        self.ws_payment_year * 10000 + self.ws_payment_month * 100 + 1
    )

    return AmortizationEntry(
        payment_num=amort_payment_num,
        payment_amt=amort_payment_amt,
        interest=amort_interest,
        principal=amort_principal,
        escrow=amort_escrow if self.loan_mortgage else Decimal("0.00"),
        total_pmt=amort_total_pmt,
        balance=amort_balance,
        payment_date=amort_payment_date,
    )

def advance_payment_date(self):
    """10660-ADVANCE-PAYMENT-DATE."""
    self.ws_payment_month += 1
    if self.ws_payment_month > 12:
        self.ws_payment_month = 1
        self.ws_payment_year += 1

def finalize_loan(self):
    """10700-FINALIZE-LOAN."""
    self.ws_loan_start_date = date.today()
    self.ws_loan_end_date = (
        self.ws_loan_start_date.toordinal() + (self.ws_loan_term_months * 30)
    )
    self.ws_loan_status = 'A'
    self.create_loan_record()
    self.disburse_funds()
    self.send_confirmation()

def create_loan_record(self):
    """10750-CREATE-LOAN-RECORD."""
    self.loan_rec_id = self.ws_loan_id
    self.loan_rec_type = self.ws_loan_type
    self.loan_rec_amount = self.ws_loan_amount
    self.loan_rec_rate = self.ws_loan_interest_rate
    self.loan_rec_payment = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    self.loan_rec_start = self.ws_loan_start_date
    self.loan_rec_status = self.ws_loan_status
    self.loan_record = (
        self.loan_rec_id,
        self.loan_rec_type,
        self.loan_rec_amount,
        self.loan_rec_rate,
        self.loan_rec_payment,
        self.loan_rec_start,
        self.loan_rec_status,
    )

def disburse_funds(self):
    """10760-DISBURSE-FUNDS."""
    self.ws_disbursement_amount = self.ws_loan_amount
    self.process_deposit()
    self.write_audit_trail()

def send_confirmation(self):
    """10770-SEND-CONFIRMATION."""
    self.ws_notif_type = 'LOAN-CONFIRM'
    self.ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    self.ws_notif_subject = 'Your loan has been approved'
    self.send_notification()

def process_decline(self):
    """10800-PROCESS-DECLINE."""
    self.ws_loan_status = 'DECLINED'
    self.record_decline()
    self.send_decline_notice()

def record_decline(self):
    """10810-RECORD-DECLINE."""
    self.decline_loan_id = self.ws_loan_id
    self.decline_status = self.ws_approval_status
    self.decline_reason = self.ws_conditions
    self.decline_date = date.today()
    self.decline_record = (
        self.decline_loan_id,
        self.decline_status,
        self.decline_reason,
        self.decline_date,
    )

def send_decline_notice(self):
    """10820-SEND-DECLINE-NOTICE."""
    self.ws_notif_type = 'LOAN-DECLINE'
    self.ws_notif_channel = 'LETTER'
    self.ws_notif_subject = 'Regarding your loan application'
    self.send_notification()

def portfolio_management(self):
    """11000-PORTFOLIO-MANAGEMENT."""
    self.load_portfolio()
    self.update_market_prices()
    self.calculate_values()
    self.rebalance_check()
    self.generate_statements()

def load_portfolio(self):
    """11100-LOAD-PORTFOLIO."""
    self.ws_hold_idx = 1
    self.holdings = []
    while self.ws_hold_idx <= 100 and self.ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            holding_rec = self.holdings_file[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
            self.ws_holding = holding_rec
            self.holdings.append(self.ws_holding)
            self.ws_hold_idx += 1
        except IndexError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_holdings_count = self.ws_hold_idx - 1

def update_market_prices(self):
    """11200-UPDATE-MARKET-PRICES."""
    for self.ws_hold_idx in range(1, self.ws_holdings_count + 1):
        self.ws_quote_symbol = self.hold_symbol[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
        self.get_quote()
        self.hold_current_price[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] = self.ws_quote_price

def get_quote(self):
    """11250-GET-QUOTE."""
    self.quote_request_symbol = self.ws_quote_symbol
    quote_response = self.getquote(self.quote_request_symbol)
    if quote_response.status == 'OK':
        self.ws_quote_price = quote_response.last_price
    else:
        self.ws_quote_price = Decimal("0.00")

def calculate_values(self):
    """11300-CALCULATE-VALUES."""
    self.ws_total_value = Decimal("0.00")
    self.ws_cost_basis = Decimal("0.00")
    self.ws_unrealized_gain = Decimal("0.00")
    for self.ws_hold_idx in range(1, self.ws_holdings_count + 1):
        self.calculate_holding_value()

def calculate_holding_value(self):
    """11350-CALCULATE-HOLDING-VALUE."""
    self.hold_market_value[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] = (
        self.hold_shares[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
        * self.hold_current_price[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
    )
    self.ws_hold_cost = (
        self.hold_shares[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
        * self.hold_cost_per_share[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
    )
    self.hold_gain_loss[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] = (
        self.hold_market_value[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] - self.ws_hold_cost
    )
    if self.ws_hold_cost > 0:
        self.hold_pct_change[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] = (
            self.hold_gain_loss[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] / self.ws_hold_cost
        ) * 100
    else:
        self.hold_pct_change[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] = Decimal("0.00")
    self.ws_total_value += self.hold_market_value[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
    self.ws_cost_basis += self.ws_hold_cost
    self.ws_unrealized_gain += self.hold_gain_loss[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]

def rebalance_check(self):
    """11400-REBALANCE-CHECK."""
    self.calculate_current_allocation()
    self.compare_to_target()
    if self.ws_rebalance_needed == 'Y':
        self.generate_rebalance_trades()

def calculate_current_allocation(self):
    """11410-CALCULATE-CURRENT-ALLOCATION."""
    self.ws_stocks_value = Decimal("0.00")
    self.ws_bonds_value = Decimal("0.00")
    self.ws_cash_value = Decimal("0.00")
    for self.ws_hold_idx in range(1, self.ws_holdings_count + 1):
        match self.hold_type[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]:
            case 'STK':
                self.ws_stocks_value += self.hold_market_value[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
            case 'BND':
                self.ws_bonds_value += self.hold_market_value[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
            case 'CSH':
                self.ws_cash_value += self.hold_market_value[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
    self.ws_stocks_pct = (self.ws_stocks_value / self.ws_total_value) * 100
    self.ws_bonds_pct = (self.ws_bonds_value / self.ws_total_value) * 100
    self.ws_cash_pct = (self.ws_cash_value / self.ws_total_value) * 100

def compare_to_target(self):
    """11420-COMPARE-TO-TARGET."""
    self.ws_rebalance_needed = 'N'
    self.ws_stocks_diff = self.ws_stocks_pct - self.ws_target_stocks_pct
    self.ws_bonds_diff = self.ws_bonds_pct - self.ws_target_bonds_pct
    if abs(self.ws_stocks_diff) > 5:
        self.ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'
    if abs(self.ws_bonds_diff) > 5:
        self.ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'

def generate_rebalance_trades(self):
    """11430-GENERATE-REBALANCE-TRADES."""
    if self.ws_stocks_diff > 0:
        self.ws_sell_amount = self.ws_total_value * self.ws_stocks_diff / 100
        self.create_sell_order()
    else:
        self.ws_buy_amount = self.ws_total_value * (0 - self.ws_stocks_diff) / 100
        self.create_buy_order()

def create_sell_order(self):
    """11440-CREATE-SELL-ORDER."""
    self.ws_trade_type = 'SELL'
    self.ws_order_type = 'MARKET'
    self.ws_trade_amount = self.ws_sell_amount
    self.trade_execution()

def create_buy_order(self):
    """11450-CREATE-BUY-ORDER."""
    self.ws_trade_type = 'BUY '
    self.ws_order_type = 'MARKET'
    self.ws_trade_amount = self.ws_buy_amount
    self.trade_execution()

def generate_statements(self):
    """11500-GENERATE-STATEMENTS."""
    self.monthly_statement()
    if self.ws_end_of_quarter == 'Y':
        self.quarterly_report()
    if self.ws_end_of_year == 'Y':
        self.annual_tax_report()

def monthly_statement(self):
    """11510-MONTHLY-STATEMENT."""
    self.rpt_title = 'MONTHLY INVESTMENT STATEMENT'
    self.write_holdings_detail()

def write_holdings_detail(self):
    """11515-WRITE-HOLDINGS-DETAIL."""
    for self.ws_hold_idx in range(1, self.ws_holdings_count + 1):
        self.rpt_symbol = self.hold_symbol[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
        self.rpt_shares = self.hold_shares[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
        self.rpt_price = self.hold_current_price[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
        self.rpt_value = self.hold_market_value[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
        self.rpt_gain = self.hold_gain_loss[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
        self.report_record = (
            self.rpt_symbol,
            self.rpt_shares,
            self.rpt_price,
            self.rpt_value,
            self.rpt_gain,
        )

def quarterly_report(self):
    """11520-QUARTERLY-REPORT."""
    self.rpt_title = 'QUARTERLY PERFORMANCE REPORT'
    self.rpt_quarter_return = (
        (self.ws_total_value - self.ws_quarter_start_value)
        / self.ws_quarter_start_value
    ) * 100
    self.report_record = (self.rpt_quarter_return,)

def annual_tax_report(self):
    """11530-ANNUAL-TAX-REPORT."""
    self.rpt_title = 'ANNUAL TAX REPORT - 1099'
    self.rpt_dividends = self.ws_dividend_income
    self.rpt_cap_gains = self.ws_realized_gain_ytd
    self.report_record = (self.rpt_dividends, self.rpt_cap_gains)

def trade_execution(self):
    """12000-TRADE-EXECUTION."""
    self.validate_order()
    if self.ws_order_valid == 'Y':
        self.check_funds_shares()
        if self.ws_sufficient_flag == 'Y':
            self.route_order()
            self.execute_order()
            self.settle_trade()
        else:
            self.reject_order()

def validate_order(self):
    """12100-VALIDATE-ORDER."""
    self.ws_order_valid = 'Y'
    if not self.ws_trade_symbol:
        self.ws_order_valid = 'N'
        self.ws_reject_reason = 'SYMBOL REQUIRED'
        return
    if self.ws_trade_shares <= 0:
        self.ws_order_valid = 'N'
        self.ws_reject_reason = 'INVALID QUANTITY'
        return
    if self.order_limit or self.order_stop_limit:
        if self.ws_limit_price <= 0:
            self.ws_order_valid = 'N'
            self.ws_reject_reason = 'LIMIT PRICE REQUIRED'

def check_funds_shares(self):
    """12200-CHECK-FUNDS-SHARES."""
    self.ws_sufficient_flag = 'Y'
    if self.trade_buy:
        self.ws_required_funds = (
            self.ws_trade_shares * self.ws_estimated_price
        )
        if self.ws_required_funds > self.ws_available_cash:
            self.ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            self.ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if self.trade_sell:
        self.check_share_position()
        if self.ws_current_shares < self.ws_trade_shares:
            self.ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            self.ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT SHARES'

def check_share_position(self):
    """12250-CHECK-SHARE-POSITION."""
    self.ws_current_shares = Decimal("0.00")
    for self.ws_hold_idx in range(1, self.ws_holdings_count + 1):
        if self.hold_symbol[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] == self.ws_trade_symbol:
            self.ws_current_shares += self.hold_shares[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]

def route_order(self):
    """12300-ROUTE-ORDER."""
    match True:
        case self.ws_trade_amount > 100000:
            self.ws_routing_type = 'ALGO'
        case self.ws_trade_amount > 10000:
            self.ws_routing_type = 'SMART'
        case _:
            self.ws_routing_type = 'DIRECT'
    self.ws_order_time = date.today()

def execute_order(self):
    """12400-EXECUTE-ORDER."""
    if self.order_market:
        self.market_order()
    elif self.order_limit:
        self.limit_order()
    elif self.order_stop:
        self.stop_order()
    else:
        self.stop_limit_order()

def market_order(self):
    """12410-MARKET-ORDER."""
    self.ws_executed_price = self.ws_current_market_price
    self.ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
    self.ws_execution_time = date.today()

def limit_order(self):
    """12420-LIMIT-ORDER."""
    if self.trade_buy:
        if self.ws_current_market_price <= self.ws_limit_price:
            self.ws_executed_price = self.ws_current_market_price
            self.ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            self.ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'
    else:
        if self.ws_current_market_price >= self.ws_limit_price:
            self.ws_executed_price = self.ws_current_market_price
            self.ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            self.ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def stop_order(self):
    """12430-STOP-ORDER."""
    if self.trade_sell:
        if self.ws_current_market_price <= self.ws_stop_price:
            self.ws_executed_price = self.ws_current_market_price
            self.ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            self.ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def stop_limit_order(self):
    """12440-STOP-LIMIT-ORDER."""
    if self.ws_current_market_price <= self.ws_stop_price:
        self.limit_order()
    else:
        self.ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def settle_trade(self):
    """12500-SETTLE-TRADE."""
    if self.ws_trade_status == 'FILLED':
        self.calculate_costs()
        self.update_positions()
        self.update_cash()
        self.record_trade()

def calculate_costs(self):
    """12510-CALCULATE-COSTS."""
    self.ws_gross_amount = self.ws_trade_shares * self.ws_executed_price
    match True:
        case self.ws_gross_amount > 100000:
            self.ws_commission = self.ws_gross_amount * Decimal("0.0005")
        case self.ws_gross_amount > 10000:
            self.ws_commission = self.ws_gross_amount * Decimal("0.001")
        case _:
            self.ws_commission = Decimal("4.95")
    self.ws_fees = self.ws_gross_amount * Decimal("0.00002")
    if self.trade_buy:
        self.ws_net_amount = (
            self.ws_gross_amount + self.ws_commission + self.ws_fees
        )
    else:
        self.ws_net_amount = (
            self.ws_gross_amount - self.ws_commission - self.ws_fees
        )

def update_positions(self):
    """12520-UPDATE-POSITIONS."""
    if self.trade_buy:
        self.add_to_position()
    else:
        self.reduce_position()

def add_to_position(self):
    """12525-ADD-TO-POSITION."""
    self.ws_hold_idx = 1
    found = False
    for i, holding in enumerate(self.holdings):
        if self.hold_symbol[i] == self.ws_trade_symbol:
            self.ws_hold_idx = i + 1
            self.ws_new_total_shares = (
                self.hold_shares[i] + self.ws_trade_shares
            )
            self.ws_new_cost = (
                self.hold_shares[i] * self.hold_cost_per_share[i]
            ) + (self.ws_trade_shares * self.ws_executed_price)
            self.hold_cost_per_share[i] = (
                self.ws_new_cost / self.ws_new_total_shares
            )
            self.hold_shares[i] = self.ws_new_total_shares
            found = True
            break
    if not found:
        self.create_new_position()

def reduce_position(self):
    """12526-REDUCE-POSITION."""
    self.ws_hold_idx = 1
    found = False
    for i, holding in enumerate(self.holdings):
        if self.hold_symbol[i] == self.ws_trade_symbol:
            self.ws_hold_idx = i + 1
            self.hold_shares[i] -= self.ws_trade_shares
            self.ws_realized_gain = self.ws_trade_shares * (
                self.ws_executed_price - self.hold_cost_per_share[i]
            )
            self.ws_realized_gain_ytd += self.ws_realized_gain
            found = True
            break

def create_new_position(self):
    """12527-CREATE-NEW-POSITION."""
    self.ws_holdings_count += 1
    self.hold_symbol.append(self.ws_trade_symbol)
    self.hold_shares.append(self.ws_trade_shares)
    self.hold_cost_per_share.append(self.ws_executed_price)
    self.hold_current_price.append(self.ws_executed_price)
    self.hold_purchase_date.append(date.today())

def update_cash(self):
    """12530-UPDATE-CASH."""
    if self.trade_buy:
        self.ws_available_cash -= self.ws_net_amount
    else:
        self.ws_available_cash += self.ws_net_amount

def record_trade(self):
    """12540-RECORD-TRADE."""
    self.trade_rec_id = self.ws_trade_id
    self.trade_rec_type = self.ws_trade_type
    self.trade_rec_symbol = self.ws_trade_symbol
    self.trade_rec_shares = self.ws_trade_shares
    self.trade_rec_price = self.ws_executed_price
    self.trade_rec_comm = self.ws_commission
    self.trade_rec_net = self.ws_net_amount
    self.trade_rec_time = self.ws_execution_time
    self.trade_record = (
        self.trade_rec_id,
        self.trade_rec_type,
        self.trade_rec_symbol,
        self.trade_rec_shares,
        self.trade_rec_price,
        self.trade_rec_comm,
        self.trade_rec_net,
        self.trade_rec_time,
    )

def reject_order(self):
    """12600-REJECT-ORDER."""
    self.ws_trade_status = 'REJECTED'
    self.reject_order_id = self.ws_trade_id
    self.reject_reason = self.ws_reject_reason
    self.reject_date = date.today()
    self.reject_record = (self.reject_order_id, self.reject_reason, self.reject_date)

def insurance_processing(self):
    """13000-INSURANCE-PROCESSING."""
    self.validate_policy()
    self.calculate_premium()
    self.underwriting()
    self.issue_policy()
    self.claims_handling()

def validate_policy(self):
    """13100-VALIDATE-POLICY."""
    self.ws_valid_flag = 'Y'
    if self.ws_coverage_amount < 1000:
        self.ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        self.ws_error_msg = 'MINIMUM COVERAGE NOT MET'
    if self.ws_effective_date < date.today():
        self.ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        self.ws_error_msg = 'INVALID EFFECTIVE DATE'

def calculate_premium(self):
    """13200-CALCULATE-PREMIUM."""
    match True:
        case self.policy_life:
            self.calc_life_premium()
        case self.policy_auto:
            self.calc_auto_premium()
        case self.policy_home:
            self.calc_home_premium()
        case self.policy_health:
            self.calc_health_premium()

def calc_life_premium(self):
    """13210-CALC-LIFE-PREMIUM."""
    self.ws_base_premium = self.ws_coverage_amount * Decimal("0.005")
    match True:
        case self.ws_insured_age < 30:
            self.ws_base_premium *= Decimal("0.8")
        case self.ws_insured_age < 40:
            self.ws_base_premium *= 1
        case self.ws_insured_age < 50:
            self.ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.5")
        case self.ws_insured_age < 60:
            self.ws_base_premium *= 2
        case _:
            self.ws_base_premium *= 3
    if self.ws_smoker_flag == 'Y':
        self.ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.5")
    self.ws_annual_premium = self.ws_base_premium
    self.ws_monthly_premium = self.ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_auto_premium(self):
    """13220
