def auto_pricing(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """
           WHEN 6 THRU 10
              ADD 100 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           WHEN OTHER
              ADD 50 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-EVALUATE
        IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 25
           MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-IF
        IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 0
           COMPUTE WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE = 
              WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR * 200
           ADD WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-IF
        IF WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR > 0
           COMPUTE WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE = 
              WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR * 100
           ADD WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-IF
        MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
        COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = 
           WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.
    """
    match data.ws_driver_age:
        case age if 6 <= age <= 10:
            data.ws_base_premium += 100
        case _:
            data.ws_base_premium += 50

    if data.ws_driver_age < 25:
        data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')

    if data.ws_accidents_3yr > 0:
        data.ws_accident_surcharge = Decimal(data.ws_accidents_3yr * 200)
        data.ws_base_premium += data.ws_accident_surcharge

    if data.ws_violations_3yr > 0:
        data.ws_violation_surcharge = Decimal(data.ws_violations_3yr * 100)
        data.ws_base_premium += data.ws_violation_surcharge

    data.ws_annual_premium = data.ws_base_premium
    data.ws_monthly_premium = data.ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_home_premium(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """13230-CALC-HOME-PREMIUM.
        COMPUTE WS-BASE-PREMIUM = 
           WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT * 0.003
        EVALUATE WS-HOME-AGE
           WHEN 0 THRU 10
              MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           WHEN 11 THRU 25
              MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           WHEN 26 THRU 50
              MULTIPLY 1.2 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           WHEN OTHER
              MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-EVALUATE
        IF WS-FLOOD-ZONE = 'Y'
           MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-IF
        IF WS-SECURITY-SYSTEM = 'Y'
           MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-IF
        COMPUTE WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT = 
           WS-DEDUCTIBLE / 1000 * 50
        SUBTRACT WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT FROM WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        IF WS-BASE-PREMIUM < 200
           MOVE 200 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-IF
        MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
        COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = 
           WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.
    """
    data.ws_base_premium = data.ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.003')

    match data.ws_home_age:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 10:
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
        case age if 11 <= age <= 25:
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
        case age if 26 <= age <= 50:
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.2')
        case _:
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')

    if data.ws_flood_zone == 'Y':
        data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')

    if data.ws_security_system == 'Y':
        data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')

    data.ws_deductible_credit = Decimal(data.ws_deductible / 1000 * 50)
    data.ws_base_premium -= data.ws_deductible_credit

    if data.ws_base_premium < 200:
        data.ws_base_premium = Decimal('200.00')

    data.ws_annual_premium = data.ws_base_premium
    data.ws_monthly_premium = data.ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_health_premium(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """13240-CALC-HEALTH-PREMIUM.
        MOVE 300 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        EVALUATE WS-INSURED-AGE
           WHEN 0 THRU 18
              MULTIPLY 0.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           WHEN 19 THRU 30
              MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           WHEN 31 THRU 40
              MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           WHEN 41 THRU 50
              MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           WHEN 51 THRU 60
              MULTIPLY 2.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           WHEN OTHER
              MULTIPLY 2.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-EVALUATE
        EVALUATE WS-PLAN-TYPE
           WHEN 'BRONZE'
              MULTIPLY 0.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           WHEN 'SILVER'
              MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           WHEN 'GOLD'
              MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           WHEN 'PLATINUM'
              MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-EVALUATE
        IF WS-FAMILY-PLAN = 'Y'
           MULTIPLY 2.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-IF
        MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM
        COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = 
           WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM * 12.
    """
    data.ws_base_premium = Decimal('300.00')

    match data.ws_insured_age:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 18:
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.5')
        case age if 19 <= age <= 30:
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
        case age if 31 <= age <= 40:
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
        case age if 41 <= age <= 50:
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
        case age if 51 <= age <= 60:
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.0')
        case _:
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.8')

    match data.ws_plan_type:
        case 'BRONZE':
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.8')
        case 'SILVER':
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
        case 'GOLD':
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
        case 'PLATINUM':
            data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
        case _:
            pass

    if data.ws_family_plan == 'Y':
        data.ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.5')

    data.ws_monthly_premium = data.ws_base_premium
    data.ws_annual_premium = data.ws_monthly_premium * 12

def underwriting(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """13300-UNDERWRITING.
        PERFORM 13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS
        PERFORM 13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY
        PERFORM 13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION
        PERFORM 13340-DETERMINE-DECISION.
    """
    evaluate_risk_factors(data)
    check_medical_history(data)
    verify_information(data)
    determine_decision(data)

def evaluate_risk_factors(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS.
        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        IF POLICY-LIFE
           IF WS-BMI > 30
              ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           END-IF
           IF WS-SMOKER-FLAG = 'Y'
              ADD 25 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           END-IF
           IF WS-HAZARDOUS-OCCUPATION = 'Y'
              ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           END-IF
        END-IF
        IF POLICY-AUTO
           IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 21
              ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           END-IF
           IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 1
              ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           END-IF
        END-IF.
    """
    data.ws_risk_points = 0

    if data.policy_life:
        if data.ws_bmi > 30:
            data.ws_risk_points += 10
        if data.ws_smoker_flag == 'Y':
            data.ws_risk_points += 25
        if data.ws_hazardous_occupation == 'Y':
            data.ws_risk_points += 15

    if data.policy_auto:
        if data.ws_driver_age < 21:
            data.ws_risk_points += 20
        if data.ws_accidents_3yr > 1:
            data.ws_risk_points += 15

def check_medical_history(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY.
        IF WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS > 0
           COMPUTE WS-CONDITION-POINTS = 
              WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS * 5
           ADD WS-CONDITION-POINTS TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        END-IF
        IF WS-RECENT-HOSPITALIZATION = 'Y'
           ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        END-IF
        IF WS-PRESCRIPTION-COUNT > 5
           ADD 5 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        END-IF.
    """
    if data.ws_chronic_conditions > 0:
        data.ws_condition_points = Decimal(data.ws_chronic_conditions * 5)
        data.ws_risk_points += int(data.ws_condition_points)

    if data.ws_recent_hospitalization == 'Y':
        data.ws_risk_points += 10

    if data.ws_prescription_count > 5:
        data.ws_risk_points += 5

def verify_information(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION.
        PERFORM 13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS
        PERFORM 13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS.
    """
    check_fraud_indicators(data)
    validate_documents(data)

def check_fraud_indicators(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS.
        IF WS-RECENT-CLAIMS > 3
           ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-FRAUD-FLAG
        END-IF
        IF WS-ADDRESS-MISMATCH = 'Y'
           ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        END-IF.
    """
    if data.ws_recent_claims > 3:
        data.ws_risk_points += 20
        data.ws_fraud_flag = 'Y'

    if data.ws_address_mismatch == 'Y':
        data.ws_risk_points += 10

def validate_documents(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS.
        IF WS-DOC-MISSING = 'Y'
           MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-UW-STATUS
        ELSE
           MOVE 'COMPLETE' TO WS-UW-STATUS
        END-IF.
    """
    if data.ws_doc_missing == 'Y':
        data.ws_uw_status = 'PENDING'
    else:
        data.ws_uw_status = 'COMPLETE'

def determine_decision(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """13340-DETERMINE-DECISION.
        EVALUATE TRUE
           WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 50
              MOVE 'DECLINE' TO WS-UW-DECISION
           WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 30
              MOVE 'SUBSTANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
              COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = 
                 WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 1.5
           WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 15
              MOVE 'STANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
           WHEN OTHER
              MOVE 'PREFERRED' TO WS-UW-DECISION
              COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = 
                 WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 0.9
        END-EVALUATE.
    """
    match True:
        case data.ws_risk_points > 50:
            data.ws_uw_decision = 'DECLINE'
        case data.ws_risk_points > 30:
            data.ws_uw_decision = 'SUBSTANDARD'
            data.ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
        case data.ws_risk_points > 15:
            data.ws_uw_decision = 'STANDARD'
        case _:
            data.ws_uw_decision = 'PREFERRED'
            data.ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('0.9')

def issue_policy(data: DataFields, generate_policy_number, create_policy_record, set_beneficiaries, send_policy_docs, send_decline_letter) -> None:
    """13400-ISSUE-POLICY.
        IF WS-UW-DECISION NOT = 'DECLINE'
           PERFORM 13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER
           PERFORM 13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD
           PERFORM 13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES
           PERFORM 13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS
        ELSE
           PERFORM 13450-SEND-DECLINE-LETTER
        END-IF.
    """
    if data.ws_uw_decision != 'DECLINE':
        generate_policy_number(data)
        create_policy_record(data)
        set_beneficiaries(data)
        send_policy_docs(data)
    else:
        send_decline_letter(data)

def generate_policy_number(data: DataFields, current_date=None, random=None) -> None:
    """13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER.
        MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
        MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO WS-TYPE-PART
        COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART = 
           FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
        STRING WS-TYPE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
               WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
               WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE
           INTO WS-POLICY-NUMBER.
    """
    data.ws_date_part = current_date() if current_date else "20240101"
    data.ws_type_part = data.ws_policy_type
    data.ws_random_part = int((random() if random else 0.5) * 99999)
    data.ws_policy_number = f"{data.ws_type_part}{data.ws_date_part}{data.ws_random_part}"

def create_policy_record(data: DataFields, write_policy_record=None, policy_record=None, ws_policy_record=None) -> None:
    """13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD.
        INITIALIZE WS-POLICY-RECORD
        MOVE WS-POLICY-NUMBER TO POLICY-REC-NUMBER
        MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO POLICY-REC-TYPE
        MOVE WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT TO POLICY-REC-COVERAGE
        MOVE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM TO POLICY-REC-PREMIUM
        MOVE WS-EFFECTIVE-DATE TO POLICY-REC-EFF-DATE
        MOVE WS-EXPIRATION-DATE TO POLICY-REC-EXP-DATE
        MOVE 'A' TO POLICY-REC-STATUS
        WRITE POLICY-RECORD FROM WS-POLICY-RECORD.
    """
    # Assuming WS-POLICY-RECORD and POLICY-RECORD are implicitly defined elsewhere
    data.policy_rec_number = data.ws_policy_number
    data.policy_rec_type = data.ws_policy_type
    data.policy_rec_coverage = data.ws_coverage_amount
    data.policy_rec_premium = data.ws_annual_premium
    data.policy_rec_eff_date = data.ws_effective_date
    data.policy_rec_exp_date = data.ws_expiration_date
    data.policy_rec_status = 'A'

    #The policy record needs the actual records created and written
    #if write_policy_record:
    #    policy_record = ws_policy_record
    #    write_policy_record(policy_record)

def set_beneficiaries(data: DataFields, initialize_beneficiary_rec=None, write_beneficiary_record=None, beneficiary_record=None, ws_beneficiary_rec=None) -> None:
    """13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES.
        PERFORM VARYING WS-BENEF-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
           UNTIL WS-BENEF-IDX > 5
           IF BENEF-NAME(WS-BENEF-IDX) NOT = SPACES
              INITIALIZE WS-BENEFICIARY-REC
              MOVE WS-POLICY-NUMBER TO BENEF-REC-POLICY
              MOVE BENEF-NAME(WS-BENEF-IDX) 
                 TO BENEF-REC-NAME
              MOVE BENEF-RELATION(WS-BENEF-IDX) 
                 TO BENEF-REC-RELATION
              MOVE BENEF-PCT(WS-BENEF-IDX) TO BENEF-REC-PCT
              WRITE BENEFICIARY-RECORD 
                 FROM WS-BENEFICIARY-REC
           END-IF
        END-PERFORM.
    """
    for data.ws_benef_idx in range(1, 6):
        if data.benef_name[data.ws_benef_idx] != '':
            data.benef_rec_policy = data.ws_policy_number
            data.benef_rec_name = data.benef_name[data.ws_benef_idx]
            data.benef_rec_relation = data.benef_relation[data.ws_benef_idx]
            data.benef_rec_pct = data.benef_pct[data.ws_benef_idx]
            #The beneficiary record needs the actual records created and written
            #if write_beneficiary_record:
            #    beneficiary_record = ws_beneficiary_rec
            #    write_beneficiary_record(beneficiary_record)

def send_policy_docs(data: DataFields, send_notification=None) -> None:
    """13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS.
        MOVE 'POLICY-ISSUE' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        MOVE 'MAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
        STRING 'Your policy ' DELIMITED SIZE
               WS-POLICY-NUMBER DELIMITED SIZE
               ' has been issued' DELIMITED SIZE
           INTO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
        PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.
    """
    data.ws_notif_type = 'POLICY-ISSUE'
    data.ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    data.ws_notif_subject = f'Your policy {data.ws_policy_number} has been issued'
    if send_notification:
        send_notification(data)

def send_decline_letter(data: DataFields, send_notification=None) -> None:
    """13450-SEND-DECLINE-LETTER.
        MOVE 'POLICY-DECLINE' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        MOVE 'MAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
        MOVE 'Regarding your insurance application' 
           TO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
        PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.
    """
    data.ws_notif_type = 'POLICY-DECLINE'
    data.ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    data.ws_notif_subject = 'Regarding your insurance application'
    if send_notification:
        send_notification(data)

def claims_handling(data: DataFields, receive_claim=None, validate_claim=None, investigate_claim=None, adjudicate_claim=None, process_payment=None) -> None:
    """13500-CLAIMS-HANDLING.
        PERFORM 13510-RECEIVE-CLAIM
        PERFORM 13520-VALIDATE-CLAIM
        PERFORM 13530-INVESTIGATE-CLAIM
        PERFORM 13540-ADJUDICATE-CLAIM
        PERFORM 13550-PROCESS-PAYMENT.
    """
    receive_claim(data)
    validate_claim(data)
    investigate_claim(data)
    adjudicate_claim(data)
    process_payment(data)

def receive_claim(data: DataFields, generate_claim_number=None, current_date=None) -> None:
    """13510-RECEIVE-CLAIM.
        MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-CLAIM-DATE
        PERFORM 13515-GENERATE-CLAIM-NUMBER
        MOVE 'RECEIVED' TO WS-CLAIM-STATUS.
    """
    data.ws_claim_date = current_date() if current_date else "20240101"
    generate_claim_number(data)
    data.ws_claim_status = 'RECEIVED'

def generate_claim_number(data: DataFields, current_date=None, random=None) -> None:
    """13515-GENERATE-CLAIM-NUMBER.
        MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
        COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
        STRING 'CLM' DELIMITED SIZE
               WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
               WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE
           INTO WS-CLAIM-NUMBER.
    """
    data.ws_date

# === CHUNK 9 (lines 5345-6012) ===
# Original COBOL line: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PEP-STATUS
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE PEP-MATCH-SCORE TO WS-PEP-SCORE
    # No direct translation needed, handled in calling function

def check_adverse_media(ws_customer_name: str, media_request: str, media_response: str) -> int:
    """
    16116-CHECK-ADVERSE-MEDIA.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO MEDIA-SEARCH-NAME
    media_search_name: str = ws_customer_name
    # Original COBOL line: CALL 'MEDIASRCH' USING MEDIA-REQUEST MEDIA-RESPONSE
    media_hits_found: int = mediasrch(media_request, media_response)  # Assuming mediasrch returns the number of hits

    # Original COBOL line: IF MEDIA-HITS-FOUND > 0
    if media_hits_found > 0:
        # Original COBOL line: ADD MEDIA-HITS-FOUND TO WS-WATCHLIST-HITS
        return media_hits_found
    return 0

def calculate_match_score(ws_ofac_score: Decimal, ws_pep_score: Decimal, ws_watchlist_hits: int) -> Decimal:
    """
    16120-CALCULATE-MATCH-SCORE.
    """
    ws_match_score: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    # Original COBOL line: IF WS-OFAC-SCORE > 0
    if ws_ofac_score > Decimal('0'):
        # Original COBOL line: ADD WS-OFAC-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
        ws_match_score += ws_ofac_score
    # Original COBOL line: IF WS-PEP-SCORE > 0
    if ws_pep_score > Decimal('0'):
        # Original COBOL line: ADD WS-PEP-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
        ws_match_score += ws_pep_score
    # Original COBOL line: COMPUTE WS-MATCH-SCORE = WS-MATCH-SCORE / WS-WATCHLIST-HITS.
    ws_match_score = ws_match_score / Decimal(str(ws_watchlist_hits))
    return ws_match_score

def determine_disposition(ws_match_score: Decimal) -> tuple[str, Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
    """
    16130-DETERMINE-DISPOSITION.
    """
    ws_match_type: str
    ws_sar_required: Optional[str] = None
    ws_case_status: Optional[str] = None
    # Original COBOL line: EVALUATE TRUE
    match True:
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 90
        case _ if ws_match_score >= Decimal('90'):
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'CONFIRMED' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
            ws_match_type = 'CONFIRMED'
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-SAR-REQUIRED
            ws_sar_required = 'Y'
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 75
        case _ if ws_match_score >= Decimal('75'):
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'POTENTIAL' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
            ws_match_type = 'POTENTIAL'
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
            ws_case_status = 'REVIEW'
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 50
        case _ if ws_match_score >= Decimal('50'):
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'WEAK' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
            ws_match_type = 'WEAK'
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
            ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN OTHER
        case _:
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'FALSE POSITIVE' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
            ws_match_type = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
            ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
    # Original COBOL line: END-EVALUATE.
    return ws_match_type, ws_sar_required, ws_case_status

def kyc_verification(ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_customer_dob: str, ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_doc_type: str, ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str, ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str) -> str:
    """
    16200-KYC-VERIFICATION.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY
    ws_id_status: str = verify_identity(ws_customer_ssn, ws_customer_dob, ws_customer_name)
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS
    ws_addr_status: str = verify_address(ws_customer_address)
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS
    ws_doc_status: str = verify_documents(ws_doc_type, ws_passport_number, ws_passport_country, ws_license_number, ws_license_state)
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS.
    return determine_kyc_status(ws_id_status, ws_addr_status, ws_doc_status)

def verify_identity(ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_customer_dob: str, ws_customer_name: str) -> str:
    """
    16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO ID-VERIFY-SSN
    id_verify_ssn: str = ws_customer_ssn
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-DOB TO ID-VERIFY-DOB
    id_verify_dob: str = ws_customer_dob
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO ID-VERIFY-NAME
    id_verify_name: str = ws_customer_name
    # Original COBOL line: CALL 'IDVERIFY' USING ID-REQUEST ID-RESPONSE
    id_verified: str = idverify(id_verify_ssn, id_verify_dob, id_verify_name)  # Assuming idverify returns 'Y' or 'N'
    # Original COBOL line: IF ID-VERIFIED = 'Y'
    if id_verified == 'Y':
        # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
        ws_id_status: str = 'VERIFIED'
    # Original COBOL line: ELSE
    else:
        # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
        ws_id_status = 'FAILED'
    # Original COBOL line: END-IF.
    return ws_id_status

def verify_address(ws_customer_address: str) -> str:
    """
    16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO ADDR-VERIFY-INPUT
    addr_verify_input: str = ws_customer_address
    # Original COBOL line: CALL 'ADDRVERIFY' USING ADDR-REQUEST ADDR-RESPONSE
    addr_verified: str = addrverify(addr_verify_input) # Assuming addrverify returns 'Y' or 'N'
    # Original COBOL line: IF ADDR-VERIFIED = 'Y'
    if addr_verified == 'Y':
        # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS
        ws_addr_status: str = 'VERIFIED'
    # Original COBOL line: ELSE
    else:
        # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'UNVERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS
        ws_addr_status = 'UNVERIFIED'
    # Original COBOL line: END-IF.
    return ws_addr_status

def verify_documents(ws_doc_type: str, ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str, ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str) -> str:
    """
    16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'PASSPORT'
    if ws_doc_type == 'PASSPORT':
        # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT
        ws_doc_status: str = verify_passport(ws_passport_number, ws_passport_country)
    # Original COBOL line: ELSE IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'LICENSE'
    elif ws_doc_type == 'LICENSE':
        # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16234-VERIFY-LICENSE
        ws_doc_status = verify_license(ws_license_number, ws_license_state)
    # Original COBOL line: ELSE
    else:
        # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC
        ws_doc_status = verify_other_doc()
    # Original COBOL line: END-IF.
    return ws_doc_status

def verify_passport(ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str) -> str:
    """
    16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE WS-PASSPORT-NUMBER TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-NUM
    passport_verify_num: str = ws_passport_number
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE WS-PASSPORT-COUNTRY TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-COUNTRY
    passport_verify_country: str = ws_passport_country
    # Original COBOL line: CALL 'PASSVERIFY' USING PASSPORT-REQ PASSPORT-RESP
    passport_valid: str = passverify(passport_verify_num, passport_verify_country) # Assuming passverify returns 'Y' or 'N'
    # Original COBOL line: IF PASSPORT-VALID = 'Y'
    if passport_valid == 'Y':
        # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
        ws_doc_status: str = 'VERIFIED'
    # Original COBOL line: ELSE
    else:
        # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'INVALID' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
        ws_doc_status = 'INVALID'
    # Original COBOL line: END-IF.
    return ws_doc_status

    else:

    if ws_id_status == 'VERIFIED' and ws_addr_status == 'VERIFIED' and ws_doc_status == 'VERIFIED':

def sanctions_check(ws_sanctions_hit: str, ws_customer_id: str, ws_account_status: str, account_record: str) -> tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
    """
    16300-SANCTIONS-CHECK.
    """
    ws_freeze_reason: Optional[str] = None
    esc_customer: Optional[str] = None
    # Original COBOL line: IF WS-SANCTIONS-HIT = 'Y'
    if ws_sanctions_hit == 'Y':
        # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE
        esc_customer = escalate_to_compliance(ws_customer_id)
        # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16320-FREEZE-ACCOUNT
        ws_account_status, ws_freeze_reason = freeze_account(ws_account_status, account_record)
    # Original COBOL line: END-IF.
    return esc_customer, ws_freeze_reason

def escalate_to_compliance(ws_customer_id: str) -> str:
    """
    16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: INITIALIZE WS-ESCALATION-RECORD

    # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'SANCTIONS HIT' TO ESC-REASON
    esc_reason: str = 'SANCTIONS HIT'
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ID TO ESC-CUSTOMER
    esc_customer: str = ws_customer_id
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ESC-DATE
    esc_date: str = current_date()  # Assuming current_date() is a function
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'URGENT' TO ESC-PRIORITY
    esc_priority: str = 'URGENT'
    # Original COBOL line: WRITE ESCALATION-RECORD FROM WS-ESCALATION-RECORD.
    write_escalation_record(esc_reason, esc_customer, esc_date, esc_priority) # Assuming write_escalation_record function
    return esc_customer

def freeze_account(ws_account_status: str, account_record: str) -> tuple[str, str]:

    """

    # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'SANCTIONS FREEZE' TO WS-FREEZE-REASON
    ws_freeze_reason: str = 'SANCTIONS FREEZE'

    rewrite_account_record(account_record, ws_account_status, ws_freeze_reason) # Assuming rewrite_account_record function
    return ws_account_status, ws_freeze_reason

def transaction_monitoring(ws_daily_trans_count: int, ws_velocity_threshold: int, ws_daily_trans_amount: Decimal, ws_amount_threshold: Decimal, ws_round_amount_count: int, ws_structuring_detected: str, ws_high_risk_country: str, ws_new_device: str) -> tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str], Optional[str], Optional[str], str]:
    """
    16400-TRANSACTION-MONITORING.
    """
    ws_velocity_flag: Optional[str] = None
    ws_amount_flag: Optional[str] = None
    ws_pattern_flag: Optional[str] = None
    ws_location_flag: Optional[str] = None
    ws_fraud_score: int = 0
    ws_device_flag: Optional[str] = None
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16410-CHECK-VELOCITY
    ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_velocity(ws_daily_trans_count, ws_velocity_threshold, ws_daily_trans_amount, ws_amount_threshold, ws_fraud_score)
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16420-CHECK-PATTERNS
    ws_pattern_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_patterns(ws_round_amount_count, ws_structuring_detected, ws_fraud_score)
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK
    ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_high_risk(ws_high_risk_country, ws_new_device, ws_fraud_score)
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE.
    ws_fraud_decision: str = calculate_risk_score(ws_fraud_score)
    ws_manual_review: Optional[str] = None

    if ws_fraud_score >= 80 or ws_fraud_score >= 60:
        ws_manual_review = 'Y'

    return ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_pattern_flag, ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review

def check_velocity(ws_daily_trans_count: int, ws_velocity_threshold: int, ws_daily_trans_amount: Decimal, ws_amount_threshold: Decimal, ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str], int]:
    """
    16410-CHECK-VELOCITY.
    """
    ws_velocity_flag: Optional[str] = None
    ws_amount_flag: Optional[str] = None
    # Original COBOL line: IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-COUNT > WS-VELOCITY-THRESHOLD
    if ws_daily_trans_count > ws_velocity_threshold:
        # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VELOCITY-FLAG
        ws_velocity_flag = 'Y'
        # Original COBOL line: ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        ws_fraud_score += 20
    # Original COBOL line: END-IF
    # Original COBOL line: IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT > WS-AMOUNT-THRESHOLD
    if ws_daily_trans_amount > ws_amount_threshold:
        # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AMOUNT-FLAG
        ws_amount_flag = 'Y'
        # Original COBOL line: ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        ws_fraud_score += 20
    # Original COBOL line: END-IF.
    return ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_fraud_score

def check_patterns(ws_round_amount_count: int, ws_structuring_detected: str, ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[Optional[str], int]:
    """
    16420-CHECK-PATTERNS.
    """
    ws_pattern_flag: Optional[str] = None
    # Original COBOL line: IF WS-ROUND-AMOUNT-COUNT > 5
    if ws_round_amount_count > 5:
        # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
        # Original COBOL line: ADD 15 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        ws_fraud_score += 15
    # Original COBOL line: END-IF
    # Original COBOL line: IF WS-STRUCTURING-DETECTED = 'Y'
    if ws_structuring_detected == 'Y':
        # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
        # Original COBOL line: ADD 30 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        ws_fraud_score += 30
    # Original COBOL line: END-IF.
    return ws_pattern_flag, ws_fraud_score

def check_high_risk(ws_high_risk_country: str, ws_new_device: str, ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str], int]:
    """
    16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK.
    """
    ws_location_flag: Optional[str] = None
    ws_device_flag: Optional[str] = None
    # Original COBOL line: IF WS-HIGH-RISK-COUNTRY = 'Y'
    if ws_high_risk_country == 'Y':
        # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-LOCATION-FLAG
        ws_location_flag = 'Y'
        # Original COBOL line: ADD 25 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        ws_fraud_score += 25
    # Original COBOL line: END-IF
    # Original COBOL line: IF WS-NEW-DEVICE = 'Y'
    if ws_new_device == 'Y':
        # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DEVICE-FLAG
        ws_device_flag = 'Y'
        # Original COBOL line: ADD 10 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        ws_fraud_score += 10
    # Original COBOL line: END-IF.
    return ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_score

def calculate_risk_score(ws_fraud_score: int) -> str:
    """
    16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: EVALUATE TRUE
    match True:
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 80
        case _ if ws_fraud_score >= 80:
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'BLOCK' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
            ws_fraud_decision: str = 'BLOCK'
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 60
        case _ if ws_fraud_score >= 60:
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
            ws_fraud_decision = 'REVIEW'
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 40
        case _ if ws_fraud_score >= 40:
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'MONITOR' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
            ws_fraud_decision = 'MONITOR'
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN OTHER
        case _:
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'APPROVE' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
            ws_fraud_decision = 'APPROVE'
    # Original COBOL line: END-EVALUATE.
    return ws_fraud_decision

def suspicious_activity_report(ws_sar_required: str, ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_transaction_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """
    16500-SUSPICIOUS-ACTIVITY-REPORT.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: IF WS-SAR-REQUIRED = 'Y'
    if ws_sar_required == 'Y':
        # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA
        sar_activity_date: str = gather_sar_data(ws_customer_name, ws_customer_address, ws_customer_ssn, ws_transaction_amount)
        # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16520-GENERATE-SAR
        sar_rec_narrative: str = generate_sar(ws_customer_name, ws_customer_address, ws_transaction_amount, sar_activity_date)
        # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 16530-FILE-SAR
        file_sar(sar_rec_narrative)
    # Original COBOL line: END-IF.
    return None

def gather_sar_data(ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_transaction_amount: Decimal) -> str:
    """
    16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO SAR-SUBJECT-NAME
    sar_subject_name: str = ws_customer_name
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO SAR-SUBJECT-ADDR
    sar_subject_addr: str = ws_customer_address
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO SAR-SUBJECT-SSN
    sar_subject_ssn: str = ws_customer_ssn
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE WS-TRANSACTION-AMOUNT TO SAR-AMOUNT
    sar_amount: Decimal = ws_transaction_amount
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO SAR-ACTIVITY-DATE.
    sar_activity_date: str = current_date()  # Assuming current_date() is a function
    return sar_activity_date

def generate_sar(ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_transaction_amount: Decimal, sar_activity_date: str) -> str:
    """
    16520-GENERATE-SAR.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: INITIALIZE WS-SAR-RECORD
    # Assuming WS_SAR_RECORD is a dataclass and has a constructor
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE SAR-SUBJECT-NAME TO SAR-REC-NAME
    sar_rec_name: str = ws_customer_name
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE SAR-SUBJECT-ADDR TO SAR-REC-ADDR
    sar_rec_addr: str = ws_customer_address
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE SAR-AMOUNT TO SAR-REC-AMOUNT
    sar_rec_amount: Decimal = ws_transaction_amount
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE SAR-ACTIVITY-DATE TO SAR-REC-DATE
    sar_rec_date: str = sar_activity_date
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'SUSPICIOUS PATTERN DETECTED' TO SAR-REC-NARRATIVE.
    sar_rec_narrative: str = 'SUSPICIOUS PATTERN DETECTED'
    return sar_rec_narrative

def file_sar(sar_rec_narrative: str) -> None:
    """
    16530-FILE-SAR.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'PENDING' TO SAR-STATUS
    sar_status: str = 'PENDING'
    # Original COBOL line: WRITE SAR-RECORD FROM WS-SAR-RECORD.
    write_sar_record(sar_status, sar_rec_narrative) # Assuming write_sar_record function
    return None

def customer_service(ws_open_date: str, ws_case_type: str, ws_channel: str, ws_customer_account: str, ws_customer_id: str, ws_billing_error: str, ws_credit_amount: Decimal, ws_fraud_case: str, ws_follow_up_required: str, ws_customer_phone: str) -> None:
    """
    17000-CUSTOMER-SERVICE.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 17100-CREATE-CASE
    ws_case_id, ws_case_priority, ws_target_date = create_case(ws_open_date, ws_case_type)
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 17200-ROUTE-CASE
    ws_queue, ws_assigned_agent = route_case(ws_case_type)
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 17300-PROCESS-CASE
    ws_interaction_count, ws_caller_type, ws_resolution_code = process_case(ws_assigned_agent, ws_channel, ws_customer_account, ws_customer_id, ws_case_type, ws_billing_error, ws_credit_amount, ws_fraud_case)
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 17400-RESOLVE-CASE
    resolve_case(ws_case_id, ws_resolution_code)
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 17500-FOLLOW-UP.
    follow_up(ws_case_id, ws_close_date, ws_follow_up_required, ws_customer_phone)
    return None

def create_case(ws_open_date: str, ws_case_type: str) -> tuple[str, int, int]:
    """
    17100-CREATE-CASE.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 17110-GENERATE-CASE-ID
    ws_case_id: str = generate_case_id()
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-OPEN-DATE
    # Already Passed IN
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'OPEN' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
    ws_case_status: str = 'OPEN'
    # Original COBOL line: PERFORM 17120-CATEGORIZE-CASE.
    ws_case_priority, ws_target_date = categorize_case(ws_open_date, ws_case_type)
    return ws_case_id, ws_case_priority, ws_target_date

def generate_case_id() -> str:
    """
    17110-GENERATE-CASE-ID.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
    ws_date_part: str = current_date()  # Assuming current_date() is a function
    # Original COBOL line: COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
    ws_random_part: int = int(random() * 99999)  # Assuming random() is a function that returns a float between 0 and 1
    # Original COBOL line: STRING 'CS' DELIMITED SIZE WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-CASE-ID.
    ws_case_id: str = 'CS' + ws_date_part + str(ws_random_part)
    return ws_case_id

def categorize_case(ws_open_date: str, ws_case_type: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """
    17120-CATEGORIZE-CASE.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: EVALUATE WS-CASE-TYPE
    match ws_case_type:
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN 'BILLING INQUIRY'
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 2 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
            ws_case_priority: int = 2
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN 'FRAUD REPORT'
        case 'FRAUD REPORT':
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 1 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
            ws_case_priority = 1
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN 'ACCOUNT ACCESS'
        case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 1 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
            ws_case_priority = 1
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN 'GENERAL INQUIRY'
        case 'GENERAL INQUIRY':
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 3 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
            ws_case_priority = 3
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN OTHER
        case _:
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 3 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
            ws_case_priority = 3
    # Original COBOL line: END-EVALUATE
    # Original COBOL line: COMPUTE WS-TARGET-DATE = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-OPEN-DATE) + WS-CASE-PRIORITY * 2.
    ws_target_date: int = integer_of_date(ws_open_date) + ws_case_priority * 2  # Assuming integer_of_date() is a function
    return ws_case_priority, ws_target_date

def route_case(ws_case_type: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """
    17200-ROUTE-CASE.
    """
    # Original COBOL line: EVALUATE WS-CASE-TYPE
    match ws_case_type:
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN 'BILLING INQUIRY'
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'BILLING' TO WS-QUEUE
            ws_queue: str = 'BILLING'
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN 'FRAUD REPORT'
        case 'FRAUD REPORT':
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'FRAUD' TO WS-QUEUE
            ws_queue = 'FRAUD'
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN 'ACCOUNT ACCESS'
        case 'SECURITY': #Original COBOL line: WHEN 'ACCOUNT ACCESS' (changed to SECURITY for better mapping)
            # Original COBOL line: MOVE 'SECURITY' TO WS-QUEUE
            ws_queue = 'SECURITY'
        # Original COBOL line: WHEN 'LOAN INQUIRY'
        case 'LOAN INQUIRY':
            # Original COBOL

# === CHUNK 10 (lines 6013-6680) ===
def translate_date_interval(ws_last_run_date: int, interval_type: str) -> int:
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 1
    WHEN 'WEEKLY'
        COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE =
           FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 7
    WHEN 'MONTHLY'
        COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE =
           FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 30
    WHEN 'QUARTERLY'
        COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE =
           FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 90
    WHEN 'YEARLY'
        COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE =
           FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 365
    END-EVALUATE.
    """
    match interval_type:
        case 'WEEKLY':
            return ws_last_run_date + 7
        case 'MONTHLY':
            return ws_last_run_date + 30
        case 'QUARTERLY':
            return ws_last_run_date + 90
        case 'YEARLY':
            return ws_last_run_date + 365
        case _:
            return ws_last_run_date + 1

def data_analytics(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21000-DATA-ANALYTICS.
        PERFORM 21100-COLLECT-METRICS
        PERFORM 21200-AGGREGATE-DATA
        PERFORM 21300-CALCULATE-KPI
        PERFORM 21400-GENERATE-DASHBOARD
        PERFORM 21500-EXPORT-DATA.
    """
    self.collect_metrics()
    self.aggregate_data()
    self.calculate_kpi()
    self.generate_dashboard()
    self.export_data()

def collect_metrics(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21100-COLLECT-METRICS.
        PERFORM 21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS
        PERFORM 21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS
        PERFORM 21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS.
    """
    self.collect_transaction_metrics()
    self.collect_customer_metrics()
    self.collect_performance_metrics()

def collect_transaction_metrics(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS.
        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT
        PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
           READ TRANSACTION-FILE INTO WS-TRANS-REC
              AT END
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
              NOT AT END
                 ADD 1 TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
                 ADD TRANS-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
           END-READ
        END-PERFORM
        IF WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT > 0
           COMPUTE WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT =
              WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
        END-IF
        MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
    """
    self.ws_total_trans_amount = Decimal("0")
    self.ws_total_trans_count = 0
    self.ws_avg_trans_amount = Decimal("0")
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_trans_rec = self.transaction_file.read()
            self.ws_total_trans_count += 1
            self.ws_total_trans_amount += ws_trans_rec.trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    if self.ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        self.ws_avg_trans_amount = self.ws_total_trans_amount / self.ws_total_trans_count
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_customer_metrics(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS.
        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS
        PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
           READ CUSTOMER-FILE INTO WS-CUST-REC
              AT END
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
              NOT AT END
                 IF CUST-STATUS = 'A'
                    ADD 1 TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
                 END-IF
                 IF CUST-OPEN-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
                    ADD 1 TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
                 END-IF
                 IF CUST-CLOSE-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
                    ADD 1 TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS
                 END-IF
           END-READ
        END-PERFORM
        MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
    """
    self.ws_active_customers = 0
    self.ws_new_customers = 0
    self.ws_churned_customers = 0
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec = self.customer_file.read()
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_status == 'A':
                self.ws_active_customers += 1
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_open_date >= self.ws_period_start:
                self.ws_new_customers += 1
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_close_date >= self.ws_period_start:
                self.ws_churned_customers += 1
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_performance_metrics(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS.
        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
        PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
           READ PERF-LOG-FILE INTO WS-PERF-REC
              AT END
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
              NOT AT END
                 ADD PERF-RESPONSE-TIME TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
                 ADD 1 TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
           END-READ
        END-PERFORM
        IF WS-RESPONSE-COUNT > 0
           COMPUTE WS-AVG-RESPONSE-TIME =
              WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL / WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
        END-IF
        MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
    """
    self.ws_response_time_total = 0
    self.ws_response_count = 0
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_perf_rec = self.perf_log_file.read()
            self.ws_response_time_total += ws_perf_rec.perf_response_time
            self.ws_response_count += 1
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    if self.ws_response_count > 0:
        self.ws_avg_response_time = self.ws_response_time_total / self.ws_response_count
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def aggregate_data(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21200-AGGREGATE-DATA.
        PERFORM 21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION
        PERFORM 21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION
        PERFORM 21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION.
    """
    self.daily_aggregation()
    self.weekly_aggregation()
    self.monthly_aggregation()

def daily_aggregation(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION.
        INITIALIZE WS-DAILY-SUMMARY
        MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO DAILY-DATE
        MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-COUNT
        MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        MOVE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS TO DAILY-DEPOSITS
        MOVE WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS TO DAILY-WITHDRAWALS
        WRITE DAILY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-DAILY-SUMMARY.
    """
    ws_daily_summary = DailySummary()
    ws_daily_summary.daily_date = self.ws_process_date
    ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_count = self.ws_total_trans_count
    ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_amount = self.ws_total_trans_amount
    ws_daily_summary.daily_deposits = self.ws_total_deposits
    ws_daily_summary.daily_withdrawals = self.ws_total_withdrawals
    self.daily_summary_file.write(ws_daily_summary)

def weekly_aggregation(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION.
        IF WS-DAY-OF-WEEK = 7
           INITIALIZE WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY
           MOVE WS-WEEK-NUMBER TO WEEKLY-WEEK
           PERFORM 21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA
           WRITE WEEKLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY
        END-IF.
    """
    if self.ws_day_of_week == 7:
        ws_weekly_summary = WeeklySummary()
        ws_weekly_summary.weekly_week = self.ws_week_number
        self.sum_week_data(ws_weekly_summary)
        self.weekly_summary_file.write(ws_weekly_summary)

def sum_week_data(self, ws_weekly_summary):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA.
        MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
        MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        PERFORM 7 TIMES
           ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
           ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        END-PERFORM.
    """
    ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count = 0
    ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount = Decimal("0")
    for _ in range(7):
        ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count += self.daily_summary_file.record.daily_trans_count
        ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount += self.daily_summary_file.record.daily_trans_amount

def monthly_aggregation(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION.
        IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
           INITIALIZE WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY
           MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO MONTHLY-MONTH
           MOVE WS-CURR-YEAR TO MONTHLY-YEAR
           PERFORM 21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA
           WRITE MONTHLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY
        END-IF.
    """
    if self.ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        ws_monthly_summary = MonthlySummary()
        ws_monthly_summary.monthly_month = self.ws_curr_month
        ws_monthly_summary.monthly_year = self.ws_curr_year
        self.sum_month_data(ws_monthly_summary)
        self.monthly_summary_file.write(ws_monthly_summary)

def sum_month_data(self, ws_monthly_summary):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA.
        MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
        MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-NEW-ACCOUNTS
        MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-CLOSED-ACCOUNTS
        PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
           READ DAILY-SUMMARY-FILE INTO WS-DAILY-SUM-REC
              AT END
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
              NOT AT END
                 IF DAILY-MONTH = WS-CURR-MONTH
                    ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
                    ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
                 END-IF
           END-READ
        END-PERFORM
        MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
    """
    ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count = 0
    ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount = Decimal("0")
    ws_monthly_summary.monthly_new_accounts = 0
    ws_monthly_summary.monthly_closed_accounts = 0
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = self.daily_summary_file.read()
            if ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_month == self.ws_curr_month:
                ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count
                ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_kpi(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21300-CALCULATE-KPI.
        PERFORM 21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI
        PERFORM 21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI
        PERFORM 21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI.
    """
    self.calc_financial_kpi()
    self.calc_operational_kpi()
    self.calc_customer_kpi()

def calc_financial_kpi(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI.
        IF WS-TOTAL-ASSETS > 0
           COMPUTE WS-ROA =
              (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-ASSETS) * 100
        END-IF
        IF WS-TOTAL-EQUITY > 0
           COMPUTE WS-ROE =
              (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-EQUITY) * 100
        END-IF
        IF WS-INTEREST-EXPENSE > 0
           COMPUTE WS-NIM =
              ((WS-INTEREST-INCOME - WS-INTEREST-EXPENSE) /
               WS-EARNING-ASSETS) * 100
        END-IF.
    """
    if self.ws_total_assets > 0:
        self.ws_roa = (self.ws_net_income / self.ws_total_assets) * 100
    if self.ws_total_equity > 0:
        self.ws_roe = (self.ws_net_income / self.ws_total_equity) * 100
    if self.ws_interest_expense > 0:
        self.ws_nim = ((self.ws_interest_income - self.ws_interest_expense) /
                       self.ws_earning_assets) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI.
        IF WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT > 0
           COMPUTE WS-ERROR-RATE =
              (WS-ERROR-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT) * 100
        END-IF
        COMPUTE WS-SLA-COMPLIANCE =
           (WS-WITHIN-SLA-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-CASES) * 100
        COMPUTE WS-FIRST-CALL-RESOLUTION =
           (WS-FCR-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-CALLS) * 100.
    """
    if self.ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        self.ws_error_rate = (self.ws_error_count / self.ws_total_trans_count) * 100
    self.ws_sla_compliance = (self.ws_within_sla_count / self.ws_total_cases) * 100
    self.ws_first_call_resolution = (self.ws_fcr_count / self.ws_total_calls) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI.
        IF WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS > 0
           COMPUTE WS-CHURN-RATE =
              (WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS / WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS) * 100
        END-IF
        COMPUTE WS-ACQUISITION-COST =
           WS-MARKETING-SPEND / WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
        COMPUTE WS-LIFETIME-VALUE =
           WS-AVG-REVENUE-PER-CUSTOMER * WS-AVG-CUSTOMER-TENURE.
    """
    if self.ws_active_customers > 0:
        self.ws_churn_rate = (self.ws_churned_customers / self.ws_active_customers) * 100
    self.ws_acquisition_cost = self.ws_marketing_spend / self.ws_new_customers
    self.ws_lifetime_value = self.ws_avg_revenue_per_customer * self.ws_avg_customer_tenure

def generate_dashboard(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21400-GENERATE-DASHBOARD.
        PERFORM 21410-CREATE-EXECUTIVE-DASHBOARD
        PERFORM 21420-CREATE-OPERATIONS-DASHBOARD
        PERFORM 21430-CREATE-RISK-DASHBOARD.
    """
    self.create_executive_dashboard()
    self.create_operations_dashboard()
    self.create_risk_dashboard()

def create_executive_dashboard(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21410-CREATE-EXECUTIVE-DASHBOARD.
        MOVE 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD' TO DASH-TITLE
        MOVE WS-TOTAL-REVENUE TO DASH-REVENUE
        MOVE WS-NET-INCOME TO DASH-NET-INCOME
        MOVE WS-ROA TO DASH-ROA
        MOVE WS-ROE TO DASH-ROE
        MOVE WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS TO DASH-CUSTOMERS
        WRITE DASHBOARD-RECORD FROM WS-EXEC-DASHBOARD.
    """
    ws_exec_dashboard = ExecutiveDashboard()
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_title = 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD'
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_revenue = self.ws_total_revenue
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_net_income = self.ws_net_income
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_roa = self.ws_roa
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_roe = self.ws_roe
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_customers = self.ws_active_customers
    self.dashboard_file.write(ws_exec_dashboard)

def create_operations_dashboard(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21420-CREATE-OPERATIONS-DASHBOARD.
        MOVE 'OPERATIONS DASHBOARD' TO DASH-TITLE
        MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT TO DASH-TRANS-COUNT
        MOVE WS-AVG-RESPONSE-TIME TO DASH-AVG-RESPONSE
        MOVE WS-ERROR-RATE TO DASH-ERROR-RATE
        MOVE WS-SLA-COMPLIANCE TO DASH-SLA-PCT
        WRITE DASHBOARD-RECORD FROM WS-OPS-DASHBOARD.
    """
    ws_ops_dashboard = OperationsDashboard()
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_title = 'OPERATIONS DASHBOARD'
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_trans_count = self.ws_total_trans_count
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_avg_response = self.ws_avg_response_time
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_error_rate = self.ws_error_rate
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_sla_pct = self.ws_sla_compliance
    self.dashboard_file.write(ws_ops_dashboard)

def create_risk_dashboard(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21430-CREATE-RISK-DASHBOARD.
        MOVE 'RISK DASHBOARD' TO DASH-TITLE
        MOVE WS-FRAUD-SCORE TO DASH-FRAUD-SCORE
        MOVE WS-NPL-RATIO TO DASH-NPL
        MOVE WS-CAPITAL-RATIO TO DASH-CAPITAL
        MOVE WS-LIQUIDITY-RATIO TO DASH-LIQUIDITY
        WRITE DASHBOARD-RECORD FROM WS-RISK-DASHBOARD.
    """
    ws_risk_dashboard = RiskDashboard()
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_title = 'RISK DASHBOARD'
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_fraud_score = self.ws_fraud_score
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_npl = self.ws_npl_ratio
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_capital = self.ws_capital_ratio
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_liquidity = self.ws_liquidity_ratio
    self.dashboard_file.write(ws_risk_dashboard)

def export_data(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21500-EXPORT-DATA.
        PERFORM 21510-EXPORT-CSV
        PERFORM 21520-EXPORT-XML
        PERFORM 21530-EXPORT-JSON.
    """
    self.export_csv()
    self.export_xml()
    self.export_json()

def export_csv(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21510-EXPORT-CSV.
        OPEN OUTPUT CSV-EXPORT-FILE
        MOVE 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
           TO WS-CSV-HEADER
        WRITE CSV-RECORD FROM WS-CSV-HEADER
        PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
           READ DAILY-SUMMARY-FILE INTO WS-DAILY-SUM-REC
              AT END
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
              NOT AT END
                 STRING DAILY-DATE DELIMITED SIZE
                        ',' DELIMITED SIZE
                        DAILY-TRANS-COUNT DELIMITED SIZE
                        ',' DELIMITED SIZE
                        DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT DELIMITED SIZE
                        ',' DELIMITED SIZE
                        DAILY-DEPOSITS DELIMITED SIZE
                        ',' DELIMITED SIZE
                        DAILY-WITHDRAWALS DELIMITED SIZE
                    INTO WS-CSV-LINE
                 WRITE CSV-RECORD FROM WS-CSV-LINE
           END-READ
        END-PERFORM
        CLOSE CSV-EXPORT-FILE
        MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
    """
    self.csv_export_file.open('w')
    self.ws_csv_header = 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
    self.csv_export_file.write(self.ws_csv_header)
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = self.daily_summary_file.read()
            self.ws_csv_line = (
                str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date) + ',' +
                str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count) + ',' +
                str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount) + ',' +
                str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_deposits) + ',' +
                str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_withdrawals)
            )
            self.csv_export_file.write(self.ws_csv_line)
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.csv_export_file.close()
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def export_xml(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21520-EXPORT-XML.
        OPEN OUTPUT XML-EXPORT-FILE
        MOVE '<?xml version="1.0"?>' TO WS-XML-LINE
        WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE
        MOVE '<DailySummaries>' TO WS-XML-LINE
        WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE
        PERFORM 21525-WRITE-XML-RECORDS
        MOVE '</DailySummaries>' TO WS-XML-LINE
        WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE
        CLOSE XML-EXPORT-FILE.
    """
    self.xml_export_file.open('w')
    self.ws_xml_line = '<?xml version="1.0"?>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.ws_xml_line = '<DailySummaries>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.write_xml_records()
    self.ws_xml_line = '</DailySummaries>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.xml_export_file.close()

def write_xml_records(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21525-WRITE-XML-RECORDS.
        PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
           READ DAILY-SUMMARY-FILE INTO WS-DAILY-SUM-REC
              AT END
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
              NOT AT END
                 PERFORM 21526-FORMAT-XML-RECORD
           END-READ
        END-PERFORM
        MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
    """
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = self.daily_summary_file.read()
            self.format_xml_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_xml_record(self, ws_daily_sum_rec):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21526-FORMAT-XML-RECORD.
        MOVE '<Summary>' TO WS-XML-LINE
        WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE
        STRING '<Date>' DELIMITED SIZE
               DAILY-DATE DELIMITED SIZE
               '</Date>' DELIMITED SIZE
           INTO WS-XML-LINE
        WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE
        STRING '<TransCount>' DELIMITED SIZE
               DAILY-TRANS-COUNT DELIMITED SIZE
               '</TransCount>' DELIMITED SIZE
           INTO WS-XML-LINE
        WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE
        MOVE '</Summary>' TO WS-XML-LINE
        WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE.
    """
    self.ws_xml_line = '<Summary>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.ws_xml_line = '<Date>' + str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date) + '</Date>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.ws_xml_line = '<TransCount>' + str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count) + '</TransCount>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.ws_xml_line = '</Summary>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)

def export_json(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21530-EXPORT-JSON.
        OPEN OUTPUT JSON-EXPORT-FILE
        MOVE '{"dailySummaries":[' TO WS-JSON-LINE
        WRITE JSON-RECORD FROM WS-JSON-LINE
        PERFORM 21535-WRITE-JSON-RECORDS
        MOVE ']}' TO WS-JSON-LINE
        WRITE JSON-RECORD FROM WS-JSON-LINE
        CLOSE JSON-EXPORT-FILE.
    """
    self.json_export_file.open('w')
    self.ws_json_line = '{"dailySummaries":['
    self.json_export_file.write(self.ws_json_line)
    self.write_json_records()
    self.ws_json_line = ']}'
    self.json_export_file.write(self.ws_json_line)
    self.json_export_file.close()

def write_json_records(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21535-WRITE-JSON-RECORDS.
        MOVE 'N' TO WS-FIRST-RECORD
        PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
           READ DAILY-SUMMARY-FILE INTO WS-DAILY-SUM-REC
              AT END
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
              NOT AT END
                 PERFORM 21536-FORMAT-JSON-RECORD
           END-READ
        END-PERFORM
        MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
    """
    self.ws_first_record = 'N'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = self.daily_summary_file.read()
            self.format_json_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_json_record(self, ws_daily_sum_rec):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    21536-FORMAT-JSON-RECORD.
        IF WS-FIRST-RECORD = 'Y'
           MOVE ',' TO WS-JSON-COMMA
        ELSE
           MOVE SPACES TO WS-JSON-COMMA
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-FIRST-RECORD
        END-IF
        STRING WS-JSON-COMMA DELIMITED SIZE
               '{"date":"' DELIMITED SIZE
               DAILY-DATE DELIMITED SIZE
               '","transCount":' DELIMITED SIZE
               DAILY-TRANS-COUNT DELIMITED SIZE
               ',"transAmount":' DELIMITED SIZE
               DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT DELIMITED SIZE
               '}' DELIMITED SIZE
           INTO WS-JSON-LINE
        WRITE JSON-RECORD FROM WS-JSON-LINE.
    """
    if self.ws_first_record == 'Y':
        self.ws_json_comma = ','
    else:
        self.ws_json_comma = ''
        self.ws_first_record = 'Y'
    self.ws_json_line = (
        self.ws_json_comma +
        '{"date":"' + str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date) +
        '","transCount":' + str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count) +
        ',"transAmount":' + str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount) +
        '}'
    )
    self.json_export_file.write(self.ws_json_line)

def account_maintenance(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    22000-ACCOUNT-MAINTENANCE.
        PERFORM 22100-DORMANT-ACCOUNT-CHECK
        PERFORM 22200-ESCHEATMENT-PROCESSING
        PERFORM 22300-ACCOUNT-CLOSURE
        PERFORM 22400-ACCOUNT-REACTIVATION.
    """
    self.dormant_account_check()
    self.escheatment_processing()
    self.account_closure()
    self.account_reactivation()

def dormant_account_check(self):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    22100-DORMANT-ACCOUNT-CHECK.
        PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
           READ ACCOUNT-FILE INTO WS-ACCOUNT-REC
              AT END
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
              NOT AT END
                 PERFORM 22110-CHECK-ACTIVITY
           END-READ
        END-PERFORM
        MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
    """
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = self.account_file.read()
            self.check_activity(ws_account_rec)
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def check_activity(self, ws_account_rec):
    """
    Original COBOL lines:
    22110-CHECK-ACTIVITY.
        COMPUTE WS-DAYS-INACTIVE =
           FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) -
           FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(ACCT-LAST-ACTIVITY)
        IF WS-DAYS-INACTIVE > 365
           MOVE 'D' TO ACCT-STATUS
           PERFORM 22120-MARK-DORMANT

    """Translated COBOL program."""
