    def main(self):
        """Main entry point."""
        # Placeholder for main logic, if any.

    def move(self, source: any, destination: any):
      """Generic move"""
      destination = source

    def compute_integer_of_date(self, ws_process_date: str) -> int:
        """Dummy function to simulate INTEGER-OF-DATE"""
        return int(ws_process_date)  # Replace with actual logic

    def write(self, record_type: str, data: any):
        """Dummy function to simulate writing to a file"""
        print(f"Writing to {record_type}: {data}")

    def add(self, operand1: Decimal, operand2: Decimal) -> Decimal:
        """Add two decimal numbers."""
        return operand1 + operand2

    def subtract(self, operand1: Decimal, operand2: Decimal) -> Decimal:
        """Subtract two decimal numbers."""
        return operand1 - operand2

    def initialize(self, data: any):
        """Dummy function to simulate initialize."""
        if isinstance(data, str):
            data = ""
        elif isinstance(data, int):
            data = 0
        elif isinstance(data, Decimal):
            data = Decimal(0)
        elif hasattr(data, '__dict__'):
          for key in data.__dict__:
              setattr(data, key, self.initialize(getattr(data, key)))
        return data

    def string(self, *args, into: str):
      """Simple string concatenate"""
      result = ""
      for arg in args:
        result += arg
      return result

    def function_current_date(self):
        """Dummy function to simulate CURRENT-DATE"""
        return "20240101"  # Replace with actual logic

    def perform_varying(self, start, end):
      """Dummy function for performing a varying loop"""
      for i in range(start, end + 1):
        yield i

    def evaluate_true(self, balance):
      """Dummy true eval function"""
      pass

    def move_all(self, char: str, destination: str):
      """Dummy move all"""
      destination = char * len(destination)

    def evaluate(self, condition: str, when_true: callable, when_false: callable = None):
      """Dummy evaluate"""
      if condition:
        when_true()
      elif when_false:
        when_false()

    def move_spaces(self, data: any):
      """Dummy move spaces."""
      if isinstance(data, str):
          data = ""
      elif isinstance(data, int):
          data = 0
      elif isinstance(data, Decimal):
          data = Decimal(0)
      elif hasattr(data, '__dict__'):
        for key in data.__dict__:
            setattr(data, key, self.move_spaces(getattr(data, key)))
      return data

    def perform(self, method):
      """Dummy perform"""
      method()

    def call(self, program_name: str, using_params: tuple):
        """Dummy function to simulate a CALL."""
        print(f"Calling program: {program_name} with parameters: {using_params}")

    def check_numeric(self, value: str) -> bool:
        """Dummy function to check if a string is numeric."""
        return value.isdigit()

    def if_statement(self, condition: bool, true_block: callable, false_block: callable = None):
        """Simple if statement implementation."""
        if condition:
            true_block()
        elif false_block:
            false_block()

    def shipping_logic(self, ws_process_date: str):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            MOVE 'EXPRESS' TO SHIP-METHOD
            COMPUTE SHIP-EST-DELIVERY =
               FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) + 2
         ELSE
            MOVE 'STANDARD' TO SHIP-METHOD
            COMPUTE SHIP-EST-DELIVERY =
               FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) + 7
         END-IF
         WRITE SHIPMENT-RECORD FROM WS-SHIPMENT-RECORD.
        """
        if True:  # Replace with actual condition
            self.SHIP_METHOD = 'EXPRESS'
            self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = self.compute_integer_of_date(ws_process_date) + 2
        else:
            self.SHIP_METHOD = 'STANDARD'
            self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = self.compute_integer_of_date(ws_process_date) + 7
        self.write('SHIPMENT-RECORD', self.WS_SHIPMENT_RECORD)

    def card_blocking(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            MOVE 'B' TO CARD-STATUS
            MOVE WS-BLOCK-REASON TO CARD-BLOCK-REASON
            MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO CARD-BLOCK-DATE
            REWRITE CARD-RECORD FROM WS-CARD-RECORD
            MOVE 'CARD-BLOCKED' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
            MOVE 'SMS' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
            STRING 'Your card has been blocked: ' DELIMITED SIZE
                   WS-BLOCK-REASON DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO WS-NOTIF-BODY
            PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.
        """
        self.CARD_STATUS = 'B'
        self.CARD_BLOCK_REASON = self.WS_BLOCK_REASON
        self.CARD_BLOCK_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        #REWRITE CARD-RECORD FROM WS-CARD-RECORD  # Assuming a write operation
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'CARD-BLOCKED'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS'
        self.WS_NOTIF_BODY = self.string('Your card has been blocked: ', self.WS_BLOCK_REASON, into=self.WS_NOTIF_BODY)
        self.send_notification()

    def wire_transfer(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            PERFORM 24100-VALIDATE-WIRE-REQUEST
            IF WS-WIRE-VALID = 'Y'
               PERFORM 24200-OFAC-SCREENING
               IF WS-OFAC-CLEAR = 'Y'
                  PERFORM 24300-PROCESS-WIRE
                  PERFORM 24400-SEND-CONFIRMATION
               ELSE
                  PERFORM 24500-REJECT-WIRE
               END-IF
            END-IF.
        """
        self.validate_wire_request()
        if self.WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
            self.ofac_screening()
            if self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
                self.process_wire()
                self.send_confirmation()
            else:
                self.reject_wire()

    def validate_wire_request(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            MOVE 'Y' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
            IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT <= 0
               MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
               MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
            END-IF
            IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT > WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
               MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
               MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
            END-IF
            IF WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT = SPACES
               MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
               MOVE 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
            END-IF
            IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT > 10000
               MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CTR-REQUIRED
            END-IF.
        """
        self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
        if self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == "":
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
            self.WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'

    def ofac_screening(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            MOVE 'Y' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
            MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO OFAC-SEARCH-NAME
            CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
            IF OFAC-MATCH-FOUND = 'Y'
               IF OFAC-MATCH-SCORE >= 85
                  MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
                  MOVE 'OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
               END-IF
            END-IF
            MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK TO OFAC-SEARCH-BANK
            CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
            IF OFAC-MATCH-FOUND = 'Y'
               IF OFAC-MATCH-SCORE >= 85
                  MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
                  MOVE 'BANK OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
               END-IF
            END-IF.
        """
        self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'
        self.OFAC_SEARCH_NAME = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
        self.call('OFACSRCH', (self.OFAC_REQUEST, self.OFAC_RESPONSE))
        if self.OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
            if self.OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
                self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
                self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'
        self.OFAC_SEARCH_BANK = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
        self.call('OFACSRCH', (self.OFAC_REQUEST, self.OFAC_RESPONSE))
        if self.OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
            if self.OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
                self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
                self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

    def process_wire(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            PERFORM 24310-DEBIT-ORIGINATOR
            PERFORM 24320-CREATE-WIRE-MESSAGE
            PERFORM 24330-TRANSMIT-WIRE
            PERFORM 24340-RECORD-WIRE.
        """
        self.debit_originator()
        self.create_wire_message()
        self.transmit_wire()
        self.record_wire()

    def debit_originator(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-AMOUNT FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
            SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-FEE FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
            PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT.
        """
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE = self.subtract(self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT)
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE = self.subtract(self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, self.WS_WIRE_FEE)
        self.update_account()

    def create_wire_message(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            INITIALIZE WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE
            MOVE 'MT103' TO SWIFT-MSG-TYPE
            MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO SWIFT-TXN-REF
            MOVE WS-WIRE-DATE TO SWIFT-VALUE-DATE
            MOVE WS-WIRE-CURRENCY TO SWIFT-CURRENCY
            MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO SWIFT-AMOUNT
            MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-NAME TO SWIFT-ORDERING-CUST
            MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-ORDERING-ACCT
            MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO SWIFT-BENEF-CUST
            MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-BENEF-ACCT
            MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK-BIC TO SWIFT-BENEF-BANK
            MOVE WS-PURPOSE TO SWIFT-REMIT-INFO.
        """
        self.WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE = self.initialize(self.WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE)
        SWIFT_MSG_TYPE: str = 'MT103'
        SWIFT_TXN_REF: str = self.WS_WIRE_REF
        SWIFT_VALUE_DATE: str = self.WS_WIRE_DATE
        SWIFT_CURRENCY: str = self.WS_WIRE_CURRENCY
        SWIFT_AMOUNT: Decimal = self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
        SWIFT_ORDERING_CUST: str = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME
        SWIFT_ORDERING_ACCT: str = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
        SWIFT_BENEF_CUST: str = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
        SWIFT_BENEF_ACCT: str = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
        SWIFT_BENEF_BANK: str = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC
        SWIFT_REMIT_INFO: str = self.WS_PURPOSE

    def transmit_wire(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE
               WS-SWIFT-RESPONSE
            IF SWIFT-STATUS = 'ACK'
               MOVE 'SENT' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
            ELSE
               MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
               PERFORM 24350-REVERSE-DEBIT
            END-IF.
        """
        self.call('SWIFTSEND', (self.WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, self.WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE))
        if self.SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
            self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'
        else:
            self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'
            self.reverse_debit()

    def record_wire(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-RECORD
            MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO WIRE-REF
            MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WIRE-AMOUNT
            MOVE WS-WIRE-STATUS TO WIRE-STATUS
            MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-FROM-ACCT
            MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-TO-ACCT
            MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO WIRE-DATE
            WRITE WIRE-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-RECORD.
        """
        self.WS_WIRE_RECORD = self.initialize(WsWireRecord())
        WIRE_REF: str = self.WS_WIRE_REF
        WIRE_AMOUNT: Decimal = self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
        WIRE_STATUS: str = self.WS_WIRE_STATUS
        WIRE_FROM_ACCT: str = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
        WIRE_TO_ACCT: str = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
        WIRE_DATE: str = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.write('WIRE-RECORD', self.WS_WIRE_RECORD)

    def reverse_debit(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            ADD WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
            ADD WS-WIRE-FEE TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
            PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT.
        """
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE = self.add(self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT)
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE = self.add(self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, self.WS_WIRE_FEE)
        self.update_account()

    def send_confirmation(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            MOVE 'WIRE-CONFIRM' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
            MOVE 'EMAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
            STRING 'Wire transfer ' DELIMITED SIZE
                   WS-WIRE-REF DELIMITED SIZE
                   ' completed' DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
            PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.
        """
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'WIRE-CONFIRM'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT: str = self.string('Wire transfer ', self.WS_WIRE_REF, ' completed', into="")
        self.send_notification()

    def reject_wire(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            MOVE 'REJECTED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
            INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
            MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO REJECT-WIRE-REF
            MOVE WS-WIRE-REJECT TO REJECT-REASON
            MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO REJECT-DATE
            WRITE WIRE-REJECT-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
            MOVE 'WIRE-REJECTED' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
            PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.
        """
        self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'REJECTED'
        self.WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC = self.initialize(WsWireRejectRec())
        REJECT_WIRE_REF: str = self.WS_WIRE_REF
        REJECT_REASON: str = self.WS_WIRE_REJECT
        REJECT_DATE: str = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.write('WIRE-REJECT-RECORD', self.WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC)
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'WIRE-REJECTED'
        self.send_notification()

    def ach_processing(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            PERFORM 25100-RECEIVE-ACH-FILE
            PERFORM 25200-VALIDATE-ACH-ENTRIES
            PERFORM 25300-PROCESS-ACH-CREDITS
            PERFORM 25400-PROCESS-ACH-DEBITS
            PERFORM 25500-GENERATE-ACH-RETURN.
        """
        self.receive_ach_file()
        self.validate_ach_entries()
        self.process_ach_credits()
        self.process_ach_debits()
        self.generate_ach_return()

    def receive_ach_file(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            OPEN INPUT ACH-INPUT-FILE
            READ ACH-INPUT-FILE INTO WS-ACH-FILE-HEADER
            MOVE ACH-FILE-ID TO WS-CURRENT-ACH-FILE
            MOVE ACH-CREATION-DATE TO WS-ACH-FILE-DATE
            MOVE ACH-ENTRY-COUNT TO WS-EXPECTED-ENTRIES.
        """
        #OPEN INPUT ACH-INPUT-FILE  # Assuming some file opening logic
        #READ ACH-INPUT-FILE INTO WS-ACH-FILE-HEADER  # Assuming some read logic
        self.WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE = self.ACH_FILE_ID
        self.WS_ACH_FILE_DATE = self.ACH_CREATION_DATE
        self.WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES = self.ACH_ENTRY_COUNT

    def validate_ach_entries(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            MOVE ZEROES TO WS-VALID-ENTRIES
            MOVE ZEROES TO WS-INVALID-ENTRIES
            PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
               READ ACH-INPUT-FILE INTO WS-ACH-ENTRY
                  AT END
                     MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                  NOT AT END
                     PERFORM 25210-VALIDATE-SINGLE-ENTRY
               END-READ
            END-PERFORM
            MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
        """
        self.WS_VALID_ENTRIES = 0
        self.WS_INVALID_ENTRIES = 0
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            #READ ACH-INPUT-FILE INTO WS-ACH-ENTRY  # Assuming some read logic
            if True: # Simulate end of file
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
            else:
                self.validate_single_entry()
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    def validate_single_entry(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            MOVE 'Y' TO WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID
            IF ACH-ROUTING NOT NUMERIC
               MOVE 'N' TO WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID
               MOVE 'R03' TO WS-ACH-RETURN-CODE
            END-IF
            IF ACH-ACCOUNT = SPACES
               MOVE 'N' TO WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID
               MOVE 'R04' TO WS-ACH-RETURN-CODE
            END-IF
            IF ACH-AMOUNT <= 0
               MOVE 'N' TO WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID
               MOVE 'R06' TO WS-ACH-RETURN-CODE
            END-IF
            IF WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID = 'Y'
               ADD 1 TO WS-VALID-ENTRIES
            ELSE
               ADD 1 TO WS-INVALID-ENTRIES
            END-IF.
        """
        self.WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y'
        if not self.check_numeric(self.ACH_ROUTING):
            self.WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03'
        if self.ACH_ACCOUNT == "":
            self.WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        if self.ACH_AMOUNT <= 0:
            self.WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06'
        if self.WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y':
            self.WS_VALID_ENTRIES = self.add(Decimal(self.WS_VALID_ENTRIES), Decimal(1))
        else:
            self.WS_INVALID_ENTRIES = self.add(Decimal(self.WS_INVALID_ENTRIES), Decimal(1))

    def process_ach_credits(self):
        """
        Original COBOL lines:
            PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
               READ ACH-INPUT-FILE INTO WS-ACH-ENTRY
                  AT END
                     MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                  NOT AT END
                     IF ACH-TRANS-CODE = '22' OR '23' OR '32' OR '33'
                        PERFORM 25310-APPLY-CREDIT
                     END-IF
               END-READ
            END-PERFORM
            MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
        """
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            #READ ACH-INPUT-

    def validate_stop_request(self) -> None:
        """29100-VALIDATE-STOP-REQUEST."""
        self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y'
        if self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER == 0:
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
        if self.WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED == 'Y':
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

    def create_stop_order(self) -> None:
        """29200-CREATE-STOP-ORDER."""
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD = WsStopRecord()
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_account = self.ACCT_ID
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_check_number = str(self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER)
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_amount = self.WS_CHECK_AMOUNT
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_payee = self.WS_PAYEE_NAME
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_effective_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_expiry_date = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 180
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_status = 'A'
        self.write_stop_record(self.WS_STOP_RECORD) # PLACEHOLDER

    def apply_stop_fee(self) -> None:
        """29300-APPLY-STOP-FEE."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE
        self.update_account()
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'STOP-PAYMENT'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Stop payment placed on check #{self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER}'
        self.send_notification()

    def safe_deposit_box(self) -> None:
        """30000-SAFE-DEPOSIT-BOX."""
        self.box_rental()
        self.box_access()
        self.box_drilling()
        self.box_billing()

    def box_rental(self) -> None:
        """30100-BOX-RENTAL."""
        if self.WS_RENTAL_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.check_availability()
            if self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                self.assign_box()
                self.create_rental_agreement()

    def check_availability(self) -> None:
        """30110-CHECK-AVAILABILITY."""
        self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'N'
        for self.WS_BOX_IDX in range(1, self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES + 1):
            if self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'A':
                if self.BOX_SIZE[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE:
                    self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
                    self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX = self.WS_BOX_IDX
                    break

    def assign_box(self) -> None:
        """30120-ASSIGN-BOX."""
        self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = 'R'
        self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.BOX_RENTAL_DATE[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE

    def create_rental_agreement(self) -> None:
        """30130-CREATE-RENTAL-AGREEMENT."""
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = WsRentalAgreement()
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_box_number = str(self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX)
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_start_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_annual_fee = self.WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE[int(self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE) -1]
        self.write_rental_record(self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT) # PLACEHOLDER

    def box_access(self) -> None:
        """30200-BOX-ACCESS."""
        if self.WS_ACCESS_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.verify_renter()
            if self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                self.log_access()
                self.escort_to_vault()

    def verify_renter(self) -> None:
        """30210-VERIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'N'
        if self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_BOX_NUMBER - 1] == self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID:
            if self.WS_ID_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                if self.WS_KEY_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                    self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y'

    def log_access(self) -> None:
        """30220-LOG-ACCESS."""
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG = WsAccessLog()
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_box_number = str(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER)
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_time = self.current_time()
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_type = 'ENTRY'
        self.write_access_log_record(self.WS_ACCESS_LOG) # PLACEHOLDER

    def escort_to_vault(self) -> None:
        """30230-ESCORT-TO-VAULT."""
        self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
        print(self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG)

    def box_drilling(self) -> None:
        """30300-BOX-DRILLING."""
        if self.WS_DRILLING_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.validate_drilling_auth()
            if self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED == 'Y':
                self.schedule_drilling()
                self.notify_renter()

    def validate_drilling_auth(self) -> None:
        """30310-VALIDATE-DRILLING-AUTH."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'N'
        if self.WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS >= 12:
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
        if self.WS_COURT_ORDER == 'Y':
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
        if self.WS_DECEASED_RENTER == 'Y':
            if self.WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'

    def schedule_drilling(self) -> None:
        """30320-SCHEDULE-DRILLING."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD = WsDrillingRecord()
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_box_number = str(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER)
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_reason = self.WS_DRILLING_REASON
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_scheduled_date = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 30
        self.write_drilling_record(self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD) # PLACEHOLDER

    def notify_renter(self) -> None:
        """30330-NOTIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'BOX-DRILLING'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
        self.send_notification()

    def box_billing(self) -> None:
        """30400-BOX-BILLING."""
        for self.WS_BOX_IDX in range(1, self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES + 1):
            if self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'R':
                if self.BOX_RENEWAL_DUE[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'Y':
                    self.charge_annual_fee()

    def charge_annual_fee(self) -> None:
        """30410-CHARGE-ANNUAL-FEE."""
        self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID = self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1]
        self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT = self.BOX_ANNUAL_FEE[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1]
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT
        self.update_account()
        self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] = self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] + 10000

    def merchant_services(self) -> None:
        """31000-MERCHANT-SERVICES."""
        self.process_authorization()
        self.capture_transaction()
        self.process_settlement()
        self.handle_chargeback()

    def process_authorization(self) -> None:
        """31100-PROCESS-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.validate_card()
        if self.WS_CARD_VALID == 'Y':
            self.check_fraud_score()
            if self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED == 'Y':
                self.check_available_credit()
                if self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                    self.approve_auth()
                else:
                    self.decline_auth()
            else:
                self.decline_auth()
        else:
            self.decline_auth()

    def validate_card(self) -> None:
        """31110-VALIDATE-CARD."""
        self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'N'
        self.check_luhn()
        if self.WS_LUHN_VALID == 'Y':
            self.check_expiry()
            if self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED == 'Y':
                self.check_cvv()
                if self.WS_CVV_VALID == 'Y':
                    self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y'

    def check_luhn(self) -> None:
        """31115-CHECK-LUHN."""
        self.WS_LUHN_SUM = 0
        for self.WS_LUHN_IDX in range(16, 0, -1):
            self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT = int(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER[self.WS_LUHN_IDX - 1])
            if (17 - self.WS_LUHN_IDX) % 2 == 0:
                self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT *= 2
                if self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT > 9:
                    self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT -= 9
            self.WS_LUHN_SUM += self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT
        if self.WS_LUHN_SUM % 10 == 0:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'N'

    def check_expiry(self) -> None:
        """31116-CHECK-EXPIRY."""
        if self.WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE >= self.WS_PROCESS_DATE:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'N'

    def check_cvv(self) -> None:
        """31117-CHECK-CVV."""
        self.cvvverify()
        if self.WS_CVV_RESULT == 'M':
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'N'

    def check_fraud_score(self) -> None:
        """31120-CHECK-FRAUD-SCORE."""
        self.fraudcheck()
        if self.FRAUD_SCORE < 70:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = self.FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE

    def check_available_credit(self) -> None:
        """31130-CHECK-AVAILABLE-CREDIT."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.read_card_account_file() # PLACEHOLDER
        if self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT >= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT:
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = '51'

    def approve_auth(self) -> None:
        """31140-APPROVE-AUTH."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = '00'
        self.generate_auth_code()
        self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT -= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.record_authorization()

    def generate_auth_code(self) -> None:
        """31145-GENERATE-AUTH-CODE."""
        self.WS_AUTH_CODE = int(self.random() * 999999)
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE = str(self.WS_AUTH_CODE)

    def record_authorization(self) -> None:
        """31146-RECORD-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD = WsAuthRecord()
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_card = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_amount = self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_code = self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_time = self.current_time()
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_merchant = self.WS_MERCHANT_ID
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_status = 'P'
        self.write_auth_record(self.WS_AUTH_RECORD) # PLACEHOLDER

    def decline_auth(self) -> None:
        """31150-DECLINE-AUTH."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD = WsDeclineRecord()
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_card = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_amount = self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_code = self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.write_decline_record(self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD) # PLACEHOLDER

    def capture_transaction(self) -> None:
        """31200-CAPTURE-TRANSACTION."""
        if self.WS_CAPTURE_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.validate_auth_code()
            if self.WS_AUTH_VALID == 'Y':
                self.create_capture_record()

    def validate_auth_code(self) -> None:
        """31210-VALIDATE-AUTH-CODE."""
        self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
        self.AUTH_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
        self.read_auth_file() # PLACEHOLDER
        if self.AUTH_REC_STATUS == 'P':
            self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'

    def create_capture_record(self) -> None:
        """31220-CREATE-CAPTURE-RECORD."""
        self.AUTH_REC_STATUS = 'C'
        self.rewrite_auth_record() # PLACEHOLDER
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD = WsCaptureRecord()
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_card = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_amount = self.WS_CAPTURE_AMOUNT
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_auth_code = self.WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.write_capture_record(self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD) # PLACEHOLDER

    def process_settlement(self) -> None:
        """31300-PROCESS-SETTLEMENT."""
        self.batch_transactions()
        self.calculate_fees()
        self.create_funding_record()
        self.send_settlement_file()

    def batch_transactions(self) -> None:
        """31310-BATCH-TRANSACTIONS."""
        self.WS_BATCH_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_BATCH_COUNT = 0
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
            self.read_capture_file() # PLACEHOLDER
            if self.WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
                if self.CAPTURE_SETTLED == 'N':
                    self.WS_BATCH_TOTAL += self.WS_CAPTURE_AMOUNT
                    self.WS_BATCH_COUNT += 1
                    self.CAPTURE_SETTLED = 'Y'
                    self.rewrite_capture_record() # PLACEHOLDER

        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    def calculate_fees(self) -> None:
        """31320-CALCULATE-

# === CHUNK 13 (lines 8017-8684) ===

    def main(self):
       """Main program execution."""
       self.WS_RECON_DIFF = self.WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL - self.WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL
       if self.WS_RECON_DIFF != Decimal("0"):
           self.log_recon_exception()

    def log_recon_exception(self):
        """37235-LOG-RECON-EXCEPTION."""
        self.WS_RECON_EXCEPTION = WS_RECON_EXCEPTION() # INITIALIZE WS-RECON-EXCEPTION
        self.WS_RECON_EXCEPTION.RECON_EXC_ACCOUNT = self.WS_GL_ACCOUNT  # MOVE WS-GL-ACCOUNT TO RECON-EXC-ACCOUNT
        self.WS_RECON_EXCEPTION.RECON_EXC_DIFF = self.WS_RECON_DIFF  # MOVE WS-RECON-DIFF TO RECON-EXC-DIFF
        self.WS_RECON_EXCEPTION.RECON_EXC_DATE = datetime.now().isoformat() # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO RECON-EXC-DATE
        self.write_recon_exception_record(self.WS_RECON_EXCEPTION) # WRITE RECON-EXCEPTION-RECORD FROM WS-RECON-EXCEPTION

    def write_recon_exception_record(self, record: WS_RECON_EXCEPTION):
        """Placeholder for writing the record."""
        print(f"Writing RECON-EXCEPTION-RECORD: {record}") # Placeholder

    def intercompany_recon(self):
        """37300-INTERCOMPANY-RECON."""
        self.load_ic_balances()  # PERFORM 37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES
        self.match_ic_pairs()  # PERFORM 37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS
        self.report_ic_differences()  # PERFORM 37330-REPORT-IC-DIFFERENCES

    def load_ic_balances(self):
        """37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES."""
        self.WS_IC_COUNT = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-IC-COUNT
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = "N" # Initialize EOF flag
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            record = self.read_intercompany_file() # READ INTERCOMPANY-FILE INTO WS-IC-BALANCE
            if record is None:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            else:
                self.WS_IC_COUNT += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-IC-COUNT
                self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_COUNT - 1] = record # MOVE WS-IC-BALANCE TO WS-IC-ARRAY(WS-IC-COUNT)
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def read_intercompany_file(self) -> WS_IC_BALANCE | None:
        """Placeholder for reading the intercompany file."""
        # Simulating file read for demonstration
        if self.WS_IC_COUNT < 5: # Limit number of records for testing
            ic_balance = WS_IC_BALANCE(
                IC_FROM_ENTITY=f"FROM{self.WS_IC_COUNT + 1}",
                IC_TO_ENTITY=f"TO{self.WS_IC_COUNT + 1}",
                IC_AMOUNT=Decimal(str((self.WS_IC_COUNT + 1) * 100))
            )
            return ic_balance
        else:
            return None # Simulate end of file

    def match_ic_pairs(self):
        """37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS."""
        self.WS_IC_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_IC_IDX <= self.WS_IC_COUNT: # PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-IC-IDX > WS-IC-COUNT
            self.find_ic_counterpart()  # PERFORM 37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART
            self.WS_IC_IDX += 1 # Increment index for next iteration

    def find_ic_counterpart(self):
        """37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_FROM = self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_FROM_ENTITY  # MOVE IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-FROM
        self.WS_SEARCH_TO = self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_TO_ENTITY # MOVE IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-TO
        self.WS_IC_IDX2 = 1
        while self.WS_IC_IDX2 <= self.WS_IC_COUNT: # PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX2 FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-IC-IDX2 > WS-IC-COUNT
            if self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_FROM_ENTITY == self.WS_SEARCH_TO: # IF IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-TO
                if self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_TO_ENTITY == self.WS_SEARCH_FROM: # IF IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-FROM
                    self.WS_IC_DIFF = (self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_AMOUNT + # COMPUTE WS-IC-DIFF = IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX) +
                                        self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_AMOUNT) # IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX2)
                    if self.WS_IC_DIFF != Decimal("0"): # IF WS-IC-DIFF NOT = ZEROES
                        self.log_ic_diff() # PERFORM 37326-LOG-IC-DIFF
                    break # EXIT PERFORM
            self.WS_IC_IDX2 += 1 # Increment index for next iteration

    def log_ic_diff(self):
        """37326-LOG-IC-DIFF."""
        self.WS_IC_DIFF_REC = WS_IC_DIFF_REC() # INITIALIZE WS-IC-DIFF-REC
        self.WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_FROM = self.WS_SEARCH_FROM # MOVE WS-SEARCH-FROM TO ICD-FROM
        self.WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_TO = self.WS_SEARCH_TO # MOVE WS-SEARCH-TO TO ICD-TO
        self.WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_AMOUNT = self.WS_IC_DIFF # MOVE WS-IC-DIFF TO ICD-AMOUNT
        self.write_ic_diff_record(self.WS_IC_DIFF_REC) # WRITE IC-DIFF-RECORD FROM WS-IC-DIFF-REC

    def write_ic_diff_record(self, record: WS_IC_DIFF_REC):
        """Placeholder for writing the IC diff record."""
        print(f"Writing IC-DIFF-RECORD: {record}") # Placeholder

    def report_ic_differences(self):
        """37330-REPORT-IC-DIFFERENCES."""
        print("INTERCOMPANY RECONCILIATION COMPLETE") # DISPLAY 'INTERCOMPANY RECONCILIATION COMPLETE'

    def nostro_recon(self):
        """37400-NOSTRO-RECON."""
        self.load_nostro_statement()  # PERFORM 37410-LOAD-NOSTRO-STATEMENT
        self.match_nostro_entries()  # PERFORM 37420-MATCH-NOSTRO-ENTRIES
        self.generate_nostro_report()  # PERFORM 37430-GENERATE-NOSTRO-REPORT

    def load_nostro_statement(self):
        """37410-LOAD-NOSTRO-STATEMENT."""
        self.WS_NOSTRO_COUNT = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NOSTRO-COUNT
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = "N" # Initialize EOF flag
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            record = self.read_nostro_statement_file() # READ NOSTRO-STATEMENT-FILE INTO WS-NOSTRO-ITEM
            if record is None:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            else:
                self.WS_NOSTRO_COUNT += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-NOSTRO-COUNT
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def read_nostro_statement_file(self) -> WS_NOSTRO_ITEM | None:
        """Placeholder for reading the nostro statement file."""
        # Simulating file read for demonstration
        if self.WS_NOSTRO_COUNT < 3:
            return WS_NOSTRO_ITEM() # Placeholder
        else:
            return None # Simulate end of file

    def match_nostro_entries(self):
        """37420-MATCH-NOSTRO-ENTRIES."""
        print("MATCHING NOSTRO ENTRIES") # DISPLAY 'MATCHING NOSTRO ENTRIES'

    def generate_nostro_report(self):
        """37430-GENERATE-NOSTRO-REPORT."""
        print("NOSTRO RECONCILIATION COMPLETE") # DISPLAY 'NOSTRO RECONCILIATION COMPLETE'

    def audit_trail(self):
        """38000-AUDIT-TRAIL."""
        self.log_user_action() # PERFORM 38100-LOG-USER-ACTION
        self.log_data_change() # PERFORM 38200-LOG-DATA-CHANGE
        self.log_system_event() # PERFORM 38300-LOG-SYSTEM-EVENT
        self.archive_audit_logs() # PERFORM 38400-ARCHIVE-AUDIT-LOGS

    def log_user_action(self):
        """38100-LOG-USER-ACTION."""
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD = WS_AUDIT_RECORD() # INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
        self.WS_AUDIT_ID = Decimal(str(random.random() * 99999999999)) # COMPUTE WS-AUDIT-ID = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999999999
        self.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now().isoformat() # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_USER = self.WS_USER_ID # MOVE WS-USER-ID TO WS-AUDIT-USER
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_ACTION = self.WS_ACTION_TYPE # MOVE WS-ACTION-TYPE TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_SESSION_ID = self.WS_SESSION_ID # MOVE WS-SESSION-ID TO WS-AUDIT-SESSION-ID
        self.write_audit_record(self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD) # WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD

    def log_data_change(self):
        """38200-LOG-DATA-CHANGE."""
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD = WS_AUDIT_RECORD() # INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
        self.WS_AUDIT_ID = Decimal(str(random.random() * 99999999999)) # COMPUTE WS-AUDIT-ID = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999999999
        self.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now().isoformat() # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_USER = self.WS_USER_ID # MOVE WS-USER-ID TO WS-AUDIT-USER
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_ACTION = 'UPDATE'  # MOVE 'UPDATE' TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_TABLE = self.WS_TABLE_NAME  # MOVE WS-TABLE-NAME TO WS-AUDIT-TABLE
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_RECORD_KEY = self.WS_RECORD_KEY  # MOVE WS-RECORD-KEY TO WS-AUDIT-KEY
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_OLD_VALUE = self.WS_OLD_VALUE # MOVE WS-OLD-VALUE TO WS-AUDIT-OLD-VALUE
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_NEW_VALUE = self.WS_NEW_VALUE # MOVE WS-NEW-VALUE TO WS-AUDIT-NEW-VALUE
        self.write_audit_record(self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD) # WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD

    def log_system_event(self):
        """38300-LOG-SYSTEM-EVENT."""
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD = WS_AUDIT_RECORD() # INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
        self.WS_AUDIT_ID = Decimal(str(random.random() * 99999999999))  # COMPUTE WS-AUDIT-ID = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999999999
        self.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now().isoformat() # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_USER = 'SYSTEM'  # MOVE 'SYSTEM' TO WS-AUDIT-USER
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_ACTION = self.WS_EVENT_TYPE # MOVE WS-EVENT-TYPE TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
        self.write_audit_record(self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD) # WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD

    def write_audit_record(self, record: WS_AUDIT_RECORD):
        """Placeholder for writing the audit record."""
        print(f"Writing AUDIT-RECORD: {record}") # Placeholder

    def archive_audit_logs(self):
        """38400-ARCHIVE-AUDIT-LOGS."""
        if self.WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y': # IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
            self.move_to_archive() # PERFORM 38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE
            self.compress_archive() # PERFORM 38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE

    def move_to_archive(self):
        """38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE."""
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = "N" # Initialize EOF flag
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            record = self.read_audit_file() # READ AUDIT-FILE INTO WS-AUDIT-RECORD
            if record is None:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            else:
                if record.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP < self.WS_ARCHIVE_DATE: # IF WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP < WS-ARCHIVE-DATE
                    self.write_archive_audit_record(record)  # WRITE ARCHIVE-AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD
                    self.delete_audit_file() # DELETE AUDIT-FILE
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def read_audit_file(self) -> WS_AUDIT_RECORD | None:
        """Placeholder for reading the audit file."""
        # Simulating file read for demonstration
        if self.WS_NOSTRO_COUNT < 3:
            audit_rec = WS_AUDIT_RECORD(WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP="2024-01-01")
            return audit_rec # Placeholder
        else:
            return None # Simulate end of file

    def write_archive_audit_record(self, record: WS_AUDIT_RECORD):
        """Placeholder for writing the archive audit record."""
        print(f"Writing ARCHIVE-AUDIT-RECORD: {record}") # Placeholder

    def delete_audit_file(self):
        """Placeholder for deleting the audit file."""
        print("Deleting AUDIT-FILE") # Placeholder

    def compress_archive(self):
        """38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE."""
        print("COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE") # DISPLAY 'COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE'

    def performance_monitoring(self):
        """39000-PERFORMANCE-MONITORING."""
        self.collect_metrics() # PERFORM 39100-COLLECT-METRICS
        self.analyze_performance() # PERFORM 39200-ANALYZE-PERFORMANCE
        self.generate_alerts() # PERFORM 39300-GENERATE-ALERTS
        self.optimize_resources() # PERFORM 39400-OPTIMIZE-RESOURCES

    def collect_metrics(self):
        """39100-COLLECT-METRICS."""
        self.cpu_metrics() # PERFORM 39110-CPU-METRICS
        self.memory_metrics() # PERFORM 39120-MEMORY-METRICS
        self.io_metrics() # PERFORM 39130-IO-METRICS
        self.transaction_metrics() # PERFORM 39140-TRANSACTION-METRICS

    def cpu_metrics(self):
        """39110-CPU-METRICS."""
        self.get_cpu() # CALL 'GETCPU' USING WS-CPU-UTILIZATION
        if self.WS_CPU_UTILIZATION > 80: # IF WS-CPU-UTILIZATION > 80
            self.WS_CPU_ALERT = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CPU-ALERT

    def get_cpu(self):
        """Placeholder for getting CPU utilization."""
        self.WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = 85

    def memory_metrics(self):
        """39120-MEMORY-METRICS."""
        self.get_mem() # CALL 'GETMEM' USING WS-MEMORY-UTILIZATION
        if self.WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION > 85: # IF WS-MEMORY-UTILIZATION > 85
            self.WS_MEMORY_ALERT = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MEMORY-ALERT

    def get_mem(self):
        """Placeholder for getting memory utilization."""
        self.WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION = 90

    def io_metrics(self):
        """39130-IO-METRICS."""
        self.get_io() # CALL 'GETIO' USING WS-IO-WAIT-TIME
        if self.WS_IO_WAIT_TIME > self.WS_IO_THRESHOLD: # IF WS-IO-WAIT-TIME > WS-IO-THRESHOLD
            self.WS_IO_ALERT = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-IO-ALERT

    def get_io(self):
        """Placeholder for getting I/O wait time."""
        self.WS_IO_WAIT_TIME = 100

    def transaction_metrics(self):
        """39140-TRANSACTION-METRICS."""
        self.WS_TPS = Decimal(str(self.WS_TRANS_COUNT / self.WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS)) # COMPUTE WS-TPS = WS-TRANS-COUNT / WS-ELAPSED-SECONDS
        self.WS_AVG_RESPONSE = Decimal(str(self.WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME / self.WS_TRANS_COUNT)) # COMPUTE WS-AVG-RESPONSE = WS-TOTAL-RESPONSE-TIME / WS-TRANS-COUNT

    def analyze_performance(self):
        """39200-ANALYZE-PERFORMANCE."""
        if self.WS_AVG_RESPONSE > self.WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD: # IF WS-AVG-RESPONSE > WS-RESPONSE-THRESHOLD
            self.WS_PERF_DEGRADED = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PERF-DEGRADED
        if self.WS_TPS < self.WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD: # IF WS-TPS < WS-MIN-TPS-THRESHOLD
            self.WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-THROUGHPUT-LOW

    def generate_alerts(self):
        """39300-GENERATE-ALERTS."""
        if self.WS_CPU_ALERT == 'Y': # IF WS-CPU-ALERT = 'Y'
            self.send_cpu_alert() # PERFORM 39310-SEND-CPU-ALERT
        if self.WS_MEMORY_ALERT == 'Y': # IF WS-MEMORY-ALERT = 'Y'
            self.send_memory_alert() # PERFORM 39320-SEND-MEMORY-ALERT
        if self.WS_PERF_DEGRADED == 'Y': # IF WS-PERF-DEGRADED = 'Y'
            self.send_perf_alert() # PERFORM 39330-SEND-PERF-ALERT

    def send_cpu_alert(self):
        """39310-SEND-CPU-ALERT."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'HIGH-CPU' # MOVE 'HIGH-CPU' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL' #
